eṇpatu viḻukkāṭu taṭavaikku mēl varum kuṟiyīṭukaḷ 67 maṭṭumēyākum. (pātikku mēṟpaṭṭa kuṟiyiṭukaḷ ovvoṉṟum pattu taṭavaikkum kuṟaivākavē varukiṉṟaṉa. avaṟṟuḷḷum 112 ovvoṉṟum oru taṭavaiyē varukiṟatu). āka, inta 67 kuṟiyīṭukaḷukkum mutaṉmai tantu vācikka muṟpaṭuvatu nallatu eṉkiṟār makātēvaṉ. mottam cumār 400 kuṟiyīṭukaḷil aṭippaṭaik kuṟiyīṭukaḷ 20 eṉpatu parpōlā karuttu.

7. immuttiraip paṭaṅkaḷ cilavaṟṟai mārṣal “illasṭirēṭaṭ laṇṭaṉ niyūs” 20.9. 1924 itaḻil veḷiyiṭṭār. ac cañcikai 4.10.24 itaḻil ci.ē.kāṭ, ciṭṉi smit ākiyōr cintu veḷi - cumēriya nākarikaṅkaḷiṉ toṭarpu paṟṟik kaṭṭurai veḷiyiṭṭaṉar. cumēriyarkaḷ āriyarkaḷē eṉṟa tavaṟāṉa karuttuṭaiya el.ē.vāṭel cintuveḷi makkaḷum āriyarkaḷ eṉat tavaṟāka uṉṉittu, immuttirai vācakaṅkaḷil uḷḷavai vēta, itikāca kālat teyvaṅkaḷ, riṣikaḷiṉ peyarkaḷ eṉṟu 1925 l nūl veḷiyiṭṭār. 1931m āṇṭu veḷiyāṉa mārṣal āyvu nūlil cintuveḷi nākarikam āriyaruṭaiyatu aṉṟu eṉa mārṣal niṟuviyirunta pōtilum, eḻuttu paṟṟi annūlil oru iyal eḻutiya lāṅṭaṉ, ivveḻuttukkaḷ intō āriya moḻi eṉṟu takka ātāramiṉṟik kuṟippiṭṭār. (acōka pirami eḻuttukaḷ cintuveḷi eḻuttukaḷiliruntu tōṉṟiyavai; cintu veḷik kuṟiyīṭukaḷ ōracaic coṟkaḷ; eṉpaṉa pōṉṟa ēṟkattakka karuttukkaḷaiyum lāṅṭaṉ kūṟiyiruntār). pirāṉ nāt pōṉṟa piṟarum piṉṉar avvaḻiyē ceṉṟu iḻukkiṉar. ivviyaliṉ iṟutippakutiyaik kāṇka.

8. pacipik peruṅkaṭal naṭuvēyuḷḷa īsṭar tīvil cila nūṟu āṇṭukaḷukku muṉṉar kiṭaitta marappalakaikaḷil cetukkap paṭṭuḷḷa eḻuttukkaḷukkum cintu eḻuttukkaḷukkum uṟavu uṇṭu eṉṟu ṭi heveci 1933-l oru kaṭṭurai veyiyiṭṭār. ik karuttu tavaṟāṉatu eṉa iṉṟu aṉaivarum otukkiviṭṭa karuttu.

9. iṉṟum intiya nākarikattiṉ perumpakutikku aṭippaṭaiyāka uḷḷa (tamiḻiya) cintu nākarikap paṇpāṭṭuṭaṉ ciṟu aḷavukku intō-āriya moḻiyiṉar paṇpāṭum uṭaṉ kalanta kālamākiya ki.mu.2000-1500; maṟṟum ataṟkup piṉṉar. caṅka kālattamiḻakattuṭaṉ vaṭamēṟku, vaṭaintiyap pakutikaḷuṭaṉ irunta toṭarpu paṟṟi iyal 8l kaṇṭōm. ataṟkum vekumuntiya kālattilum (ki.mu.3000-2000 allatu ataṟkum muṉṉar) cintup pakutikkum/ tamiḻakattukkum mika neruṅkiya toṭarpu iruntirukka vēṇṭum eṉpataiyum attakaiya toṭarpukaḷaik kāṭṭum cāṉṟukaḷ, caṅka ilakkiyattilum uḷḷaṉa eṉpataiyum ār. pālakiruṣṇaṉ ‘ūr maṟṟum iṭappeyar āyviṉ’ mūlam kaṇṭuḷḷār. Journal of Tamil Studies cūṉ 2010 itaḻil “tamiḻccintu nākarikam? vaṭamēṟku intiyāvil koṟkai, vañci, toṇṭi mutaliya iṭap peyarkaḷ; caṅka ilakkiyattil oṭṭakam elumpai uṇṇuvatu paṟṟiya kuṟippu” eṉṟa ciṟanta āyvil terivittuḷḷār. avar cuṭṭum iṭappeyar mutaliya oppumaikaḷ cila varumāṟu:

(i) cintu nākarikappakutiyil (iṉṟu pākistāṉ - āpkāṉistāṉ) uḷḷa tamiḻiya iṭappeyarkaḷ; koṟkai (Gorkay, Gorkai, Gorkhai), vañci, toṇṭi, maturai (Matrai), uṟai (urai), kūṭal (Kudalgarh), kōḻi (Koli); kaḷḷūr (“perum peyarkkaḷḷūr” akanāṉūṟu 256) intiyat tuṇaikkaṇṭattil mottam 38 kaḷḷūr / kallūrkaḷ uḷḷaṉavām.

(ii) malai, kōṭu, kuṉṟu, varai, muṭi, cuṉai, ēṉal, kāṭu, kāṉal, pulai, purai, aṭavi, ā / yāṟu, kaḻaṉi, maṇal, nilam, nilai, tarai, teru, maṉai, tirai, kaḷari, pālai, curam, kuṟumpai pōṉṟa oṟṟaic col ūrppeyar (ovvoṉṟum caṟṟē tirinta vaṭivil) aṅku ērāḷamāka uḷḷaṉa.

(iii) ulakeṅkum paḻaṅkuṭippeyarkaḷ maṉṉarkuṭip peyarkaḷ, kuṟunilattalaivar kuṭip peyarkaḷ ākiyavai) perumaḷavukku ūrppeyarkaḷil iruntē peṟappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. piṉvarum tamiḻ nāṭṭup peyarkaḷiṉ caṟṟē tirinta vaṭivaṅkaḷ cintup pakutiyil ūrp peyarkaḷāka iṉṟum vaḻaṅkukiṉṟaṉa (aṇṭar, āyar, kaḷamar, kāḷai, koṅkar, tuṭiyaṉ, pāṇaṉ, maṟavar, aṟivālā, utiyaṉ, kaḷaṅkāy, karikāl (Garigal) kiḷḷi, kōtai, celiyaṉvālā, cēraṉ, cēraṉvāli, cōḷā, pāṇṭiyar, pāṇṭiyaṉ vālā, poṟai, vaḻuti, atiyamāṉ, aṉṉi, ātaṉ, utiyaṉ, kaṭṭi, kōṭaṉ, cāttaṉ, titiyaṉ, tittaṉ, paṉṉi, naḷḷi, neṭumāṉ, kōṭaṉ, pāri, piṭṭaṉ, piṇṭaṉ, pēkaṉ, matti, miñili, mūvaṉ, veḷiyaṉ.

(iv) aintiṇaikaḷil oṉṟākap pālaiyaic caṅka ilakkiyam kuṟippiṭiṉum uṇmaiyāṉa pālai desert teṉṉāṭṭil illai. cintu nākarikam tār pālaivaṉattai oṭṭiya kujarāt pakutiyilum paraviyiruntatai akaḻvāyvukaḷē niṟuviyuḷḷaṉa. antappālai potumakkaḷ niṉaivil nilaittu iruntatāl tāṉ aintiṇaiyil pālaiyaiyum cērttaṉar; atu maṭṭumalla, pālai vilaṅkāṉa oṭṭakam cārnta oru viyakkattakka uṇmaiyāṉa kuṟippum akanāṉūṟu 245 il ‘tatar veḷ eṉpu kaṭuṅkāl oṭṭakattu alku paci tīrkkum’ (vēṟu uṇavu etuvum kiṭṭāta nilaiyil pacitta elumpaiyum tiṉṉum eṉpatu poruḷ. oṭṭakam elumpu tiṉpatu eṉpatu vilaṅkiyalāḷar ēṟṟuḷḷatākum) icceyti ēṭṭil eḻutātu potumakkaḷ niṉaivilum cevivaḻikkatai / pāṭalkaḷilum neṭuṅkālam iruntu piṉṉarc caṅkappāṭalilum ēṟiyirukkumeṉpar pālakiruṣṇaṉ. (iṅku “tamiḻar varalāṟu, ki.pi.600 varai (1929) nūlil pi.ṭi. cīṉivāca aiyaṅkār iyal 5 iṉ 19m pattiyil kuṟittuḷḷa piṉvarum karuttaiyum niṉaivu kūrka.

 “vētattil varum “pañca janāh” eṉpatai paṇṭaiyōr, nam kālattavar, yārālum pontikaiyāka viḷakka muṭiyavillai. paṇṭu vaṭa intiya makkaḷum aintiṇai iṉakkuḻukkaḷākap pirintiruntatai iccol niṉaivūṭṭukiṟatu. eṉavē āriyac caṭaṅku tōṉṟuvataṟku muṟpaṭṭu vaṭa intiyāvil irunta aintiṇai iṉakkuḻukkaḷai atu kuṟittiruklām eṉṟu uṉṉikkiṟēṉ. āyiṉum tamiḻ ilakkiya aṟivaṟṟavarkaḷum “āriyattukku muntiya intiyāvukkum, āriya intiyāvukkum iṭaiyīṭaṟṟa varalāṟṟut toṭarpu iruntirukka vēṇṭum eṉpatai uṇara maṟuppavarkaḷum eṉatu karuttai ēṟpatillai”.

(v) pālakiruṣṇaṉuṭaiya mukkiyamāṉa muṭivukaḷ varumāṟu.

(1) cintu nākarika moḻi mūlatirāviṭam Proto Dravidan eṉpataṟkāṉa cāṉṟukaḷ valuvāṉavai.

(2) ūrppeyar āyvukaḷ tarum oḷiyaip payaṉpaṭuttiṉāl cintu nākarikat toṭarcciyaik kaṇṭaṟiyum āyvukaḷum, akaḻvāyvukkāṉa iṭat terivukaḷum mēlum payaṉuḷḷaṉavāka amaiyum.

(3) oru pāl cintu nākarikattiṉ paḻantamiḻt toṭarpai niṟuvum muyaṟcikaḷum maṟupāl caṅka kālattiṟku muntiya pulappeyarvu maṟṟum paḻantamiḻt toṉma marapu āyvu muyaṟcikaḷum orē nāṇayattiṉ iraṇṭu pakkaṅkaḷ tām. oṉṟaṉ nalivukaḷai nīkki, valivukaḷai mēmpaṭuttuvatu maṟṟatu ākum. (ākavē iruvakai ayvukaḷum tēvai)

10. cintu veḷi muttiraikaḷ 750 ai āḻntu ārāyntu 1934l nūl veḷiyiṭṭavar ji.ār.haṇṭar. ivar tāṉ mutaṉ mutalil avaṟṟiṉ moḻi intō āriyam alla; ōracaic col mikunta (tirāviṭa moḻi pōṉṟa) moḻiyāka irukkalām eṉṟu niṟuviyavar. cintuveḷi eḻuttē piramiyiṉ tāy eṉṟa lāṅṭaṉ karuttu ivarukku uṭaṉpāṭē. cātāraṇa kāriyaṅkaḷukku cintuveḷi makkaḷ ōlaikaḷ pōṉṟa aḻiyum poruṭkaḷaip payaṉpaṭuttiyiruppar eṉkiṟār.

11. cintu veḷi nākarikam tirāviṭaruṭaiyatu eṉṟa mārṣal muṭivai valuppaṭuttiyum, annākarikamoḻi eḻuttu tol 140 toltamiḻiyac cintu nākarikam tirāviṭa moḻi eḻuttē eṉpataiyum nērmaiyāṉa aṟiñar evarum maṟukkavoṇṇā vakaiyil pala āyvuk kaṭṭuraikaḷai iṭaiviṭātu veḷiyiṭṭavar es. hīrās pātiriyār. avar 1953l veḷiyiṭṭa ciṟanta nūl; ‘toṉmai intō - naṇṇilak karai nākarika āyvu’ eṉpatākum. ivar cintu moḻiyai muntu (purōṭō) tirāviṭa moḻiyākap paṭittuḷḷār. ivarukku muṉ ivvāyvil īṭupaṭṭavarkaḷaip pōla ivarum valamiruntu iṭamākavē iv veḻuttukkaḷaip paṭikkiṟār. ovvoru kuṟiyīṭum acaiyaiyō mey/uyir eḻuttaiyō kuṟippatalla; ovvoru kuṟiyīṭum oru col (atāvatu paṭaveḻuttu / colluruvaṉ; Logographic); vācakaṅkaḷ iyaṟpeyar alla; avai kūṟṟukkaḷum viḷakkaṅkaḷum ākum; ivaiyē avar aṭippaṭaik kōṭpāṭukaḷ. mikap perum eṇṇikkaiyil varum eṉṉum kuṟi, “māṟaṉatu”, ‘vēlaṉatu’ eṉpavaṟṟiṟ pōl uṭaimaip poruḷil varum “atu” eṉṉum
collaik kuṟippatu; cilaviṭaṅkaḷil cuṭṭup poruḷilum varum; eṉṟār.

12. paṇṭaiya eḻuttukkaḷil “olikoḷ vaṭivam” (Phonograph) allatu rīpas Rebus eṉpatil aṭaṅkuvaṉa:

a) eṉṟu mīṉpaṭam pōṭṭu “mīṉavaṉai” (aracaṉai) kuṟippiṭutal

ā) ōviya eḻuttup putir (Rebus / homophony); e.kā 

bee 	leaf 	= 	belief 
(WATER) CAN 	die date = Candidate

13. pala cintuk kuṟiyīṭukaḷai ik kōṭpāṭṭiṉ aṭippaṭaiyil hīrās paṭittuḷḷār. avar paṭittuḷḷa cila vācakaṅkaḷ:-

āṇ atu mīṉ kuṭa vāḻkkai naṇṭ āṇ
“pacupati” muttirai

āṇnaṇṭ vāl key kuṭamīṉ atu āṇ
“The Lord of the Water - jar and the Fish is the weakening and strengthening of the Lord”

kaṭavuḷ eṭ ōriṭa mīṉ pēr taḷi atu

atu taḷipēr mīṉ ōriṭa eṭ kaṭavuḷ


This is the (eight) formed God one of whose sides (forms) (is) the sprinkled great fish.
atu ari valil mīṉ

mīṉ valil ari atu

This is the weak toddy of the Minas

cintu veḷi eḻuttukku tol tirāviṭam eṉa hīrās mutaṟkaṇ tiṭṭavaṭṭamāka niṟuviyatai iṉṟu nērmaiyāṉa aṟiñar aṉaivarum ēṟkiṉṟaṉar. eṉiṉum immuttiraikaḷ perumpālum etaṟkup payaṉpaṭṭaṉa eṉpataik karutuma poḻutu hīrās paṭittuḷḷa colluruvaṉ logographic vācakaṅkaḷ perumpālum eṟkat takkavaiyāka illai.

cumēriya eḻuttu muṟai cintu eḻuttu muṟaiyiliruntutāṉ uruvāṉatu eṉpatu hīrās koḷkai (1953m āṇṭu nūl, pak 249-278). cīṉa eḻuttu muṟaiyum ōraḷavukku avvāṟē eṉkiṟār avar.

14. (i) hīrācukkuppiṉ cintup poṟippukaḷait tamiḻiya (tirāviṭa) moḻiyākappaṭitta cilariṉ vācakaṅkaḷait tantuviṭṭup piṉṉar, parpōlā, makātēvaṉ, mativāṇaṉ jīvā mutaliyavar āyvukaḷ caṟṟu virivākat tarappaṭum.

(ii) pērcarvis pōṉṟa cilar vācippukaḷiṉ mātirikaḷ varumāṟu:

vālṭar pērcarvīs (1983) ōviya eḻuttup putir (rīpas) kōṭpāṭṭiṉ paṭi vācittuḷḷār.



e.kā: 		mutalaippaṭam 	= 	mutali Chief
= 	noṟukku / nūṟu
nel payir paṭam 			= 	nelā (nilā Moon)

ivar paṭittuḷḷa vācakaṅkaḷ cila varumāṟu:

āṉ paṭukāru ārapirikai ā ampara paṭā cūr
“cūr paṭāmparaā ārapirikai paṭu kāraṉ”
“Patukaran powerful (noble) chief of the surrounding settlements”

āṉ pir (ca)āy = cāy piṟaiyāḷaṉ

cuppiramaṇiyam malaiyāṇṭi (1978) : ivar tolkāppiyac centamiḻāka muttirai vācakaṅkaḷaip paṭittuḷḷār.

kiḷaiṭ akamatu viṉṭars (1985 mutal): ivar colluruvaṉ logographic eḻuttukkaḷākap paṭittuḷḷār.

ta cey u-i miṉ-i lu aṇṇal
aṇṇal lu miṉ-i-u-i ceyta
Much righteoumess, let it shine, bring here virtue.

us pukaḷ, miṉ uss kāvēi
kāvēi uss miṉpukaḷ us
Balance blooms Gods justice - a shining glorious fate
peṉāṉ spikṉiyū cālek (pōlantu)

li maa we munnu wuri ka (kaa uuri mun wel maal)
= protection end of the world before white great man)

Ya mii mii Yiru
Ai mil mil Iru = Iru mii mii ai great heavenly heavenly king

15. cintuppoṟippukaḷ tamiḻiya (tirāviṭa) moḻi cārntavaitām eṉpatu paṟṟip paltuṟai, paṉṉāṭṭu aṟiñarkaḷiṭam oru mitta karuttu uḷḷatu – ittuṟaiyil nāṟpatāṇṭu nuṭpamākavum kaṭiṉamākavum uḻaitta piṉlāntu aṟiñar askō parpōlā, nam tamiḻ nāṭṭu aṟiñar airāvatam makātēvaṉ uṭpaṭa. (tirāviṭa moḻic cārpuṭaiyatākap palarum paṭittuḷḷa pōtilum yāruṭaiya vācippum iṉṟu varai aṟiñar ulakil ēṟkappaṭavillai. īrās, parpōlā, makātēvaṉ, nāracāv, pērcarvīs, viṉṭars, cālek, mativāṇaṉ, pūrṇacantira jīvā ākiya yāruṭaiya vācippumē muḻumaiyāka ēṟkappaṭavillai). cintu eḻuttukaḷ tamiḻiya moḻi cārntavai eṉpatu maṭṭum ulakaḷavil nallaṟiñar kaḷāl ēṟkappaṭṭuḷḷatu - kāṇka:-

sṭāṉli vālparṭ (1991): intiyāvukku ōr aṟimukam ‘pala vakaiyāṉa ātāraṅkaḷiliruntum nām karutuvatu avarkaḷ tol tirāviṭarkaḷ eṉpatum, tamiḻukkup pāṭṭaṉ muṟaiyākak kūṭiya oru moḻi avarkaḷ moḻiyāka iruntirukkalām eṉpatum ākum.”

“We assume from various shreds of evidence that they were proto Dravidian, possibly using a language that was a grandfather of modern Tamil” (Stanley Wolpert : An Introduction to India, University of California Press 1991)

jē.em.rāparṭs (2010) : pelikaṉ ulaka varalāṟu “ teṉṉintiyāvil iṉṟum vaḻaṅkivarum tirāviṭa moḻikaḷōṭu iyaipuṭaiya oru moḻiyaic cārntavaiyāka (cintu muttiraic coṟkaḷ) irukkalāmeṉat tōṉṟukiṟatu.”

It now seems at least likely that they are part of a language akin to the Dravidian tongues still used inSouthern India (J.M.Roberts History of the World, Pelican 1992)

kamil cuvelapil (1990) tirāviṭa moḻiyiyal – ōr aṟimukam “cintuveḷi eḻuttukkaḷiṉ moḻi eṉṉa eṉṟu iṟutiyāka niṟuvappaṭumpoḻutu atu tirāviṭamoḻi cārntatāka amaivataṟkē vāyppumika atikam.” (“The most probable candidate is and remains some form of Dravidian” Dravidian Linguistics An Introduction. Pondicherry 1990: Chap VI: Dravidian and Harappan) 

em.pi.emeṉō cintuppoṟippukaḷai vācittu aṟivataṟkāṉa taṭayaṅkaḷai vēṟu emmoḻik kuṭumpattaiyum viṭa tirāviṭa moḻikkuṭumpattil tāṉ kāṇa vāyppu atikam uṇṭu. (The most promising language family in which to look for clues
that might aid in their tranlation has seemed to be Dravidian” (ENCYCLOPAEDIA AMERICANA; 1999)

askō parpōlā

16. 1960 kaḷiliruntu cintu eḻuttu āyvaiyē tamatu mukkiyamāṉa vāḻnāṭ paṇiyāka koṇṭuḷḷavar inta piṉlāntu nāṭṭaṟiñar. ittuṟaiyil avar paṭaittuḷḷa iṉṟiyamaiyāta nūlkaḷai mēlē iraṇṭām pattiyiṟ kaṇṭōm. tamatu āyvu muṭivukaḷai 2010 cūṉ 25 aṉṟu kōyamputtūril ulakat tamiḻc cemmoḻi mānāṭṭil āṟṟiya cintu eḻuttuc cikkal tirāviṭat [tirāviṭa moḻit] tīrvu eṉṉum poḻivil curukkamākat tantuḷḷār. mukkiyamāṉa cilavaṟṟaik kāṇpōm.

17. tol intō - airōppiya moḻi pēcunar ki.mu. 3000kkup piṉṉar teṉ - kiḻakku airōppiyap pakutiyiliruntu (karuṅkaṭal - kāspiyaṉ kaṭalkaḷukku vaṭakkiluḷḷa pakuti) mēṟku, teṟku, teṉ kiḻakkākap paravat toṭaṅkiya poḻutu ki.mu.2300 - 1500 kālam cārnta pākṭiriyā – mārjiyāṉā tolliyal nākarikat (Bactria and Margiana Archaeological Complex = BMAC)  tolliyal eccappakutiyait tāṇṭi ki.mu.1900 – 1600 il īrāṉ - cintup pakutikku varalāyiṉar. (ki.mu 1900 – 1600 eṉpatu hārappā nākarikattiṉ kaṭaip pakutiyākum). varalāṟṟu moḻiyiyal cāṉṟukaḷ aṭippaṭaiyil (virivāṉa vivaraṅkaḷ muntaiya iyaliṉ kaṭaicip pakutiyil) – kuṟippāka rikvēta ttilēyē kāṇappaṭum tirāviṭa moḻiyiyal, ilakkaṇak kūṟukaḷ, tirāviṭac coṟkaḷ ivaṟṟiṉ aṭippaṭaiyil, hārappā nākarika makkaḷ pēciyatu tirāviṭa moḻiyākattāṉ iruntirukka vēṇṭum. (Historical Linguistics, thus suggests that the Harappans probably spoke a Dravidian language)

18. cintu eḻuttai vācikka muyalvōr palaruṭaiya piṉvarum karutukōḷkaḷ tavaṟu eṉpar parpōlā:-

(i) cintu lipiyil uḷḷa vaṭivam pōṉṟa oṉṟu vēṟu enta lipiyilum iruntāl antap piṟa lipiyiṉ oli / poruḷtāṉ cintu vaṭivattiṟkum eṉṟu tavaṟākak karutik koḷvatu.

(ii) cemittiya “meyyeḻuttaṭippaṭai vaṇṇamālai” Semitic Consonantal alphablet ai aṭiyoṟṟi uruvāṉatu piramilipiyākum. cintu lipiyiliruntu uruvāṉatalla piramilipi; eṉiṉum cintulipiyiliruntu pirami uruvāṉatākat tavaṟāka eṇṇik koṇṭu pirami aṭippaṭaiyil cintup poṟippukaḷaip paṭikka muyalum tavaṟāṉa pōkku.

19. ulakil enta acaiyeḻuttu / vaṇṇamālai varivaṭivamum (Syllabic or alphabetic script) tōṉṟuvataṟkum muṉṉar tōṉṟiya cintu eḻuttu collacaiyaṉ varivaṭiva mākattāṉ logo - syllabic writing system) irukka vēṇṭum. avai perumpālum mēlē viḷakkiya (oli oppumai homophone aṭippaṭaiyil uruvākiya) rīpas vakaic collacaiyaṉ Logosyllabic varivaṭivaṅkaḷē, tamiḻil “mīṉ” eṉpatu fish  aiyum kuṟittatu eṉka. cintu muttiraikaḷil “10 kuṟikaḷil oṉṟu mīṉ’ eṉṟa aḷavukku mīṉ uruvam (veṟum mīṉ uruvam, maṟṟum arttam māṟuvataik kuṟikkum palvēṟu aṭaiyāḷaṅkaḷaik diacritical marks koṇṭa uruvaṅkaḷ) kāṇappaṭukiṟatu.

20. tamiḻ vaṭam / vaṭamaram (=ālamaram) eṉpatil varum ‘vaṭa’ kayiṟaiyum kuṟittatu. (kayiṟu pōṉṟatu ālamara viḻutu, eṉavē) vaṭaticaiyaiyum kuṟittatu, “vaṭa mīṉ’ vaṭaticaiyil uḷḷa turuva naṭcattirattaik kuṟittatu. pōṉṟa ālamarat tōṟṟam koṇṭa cintu vaṭivaṅkaḷ oli oppumai aṭippaṭaiyil ‘vaṭa’ eṉpataik kuṟittaṉa. tamiḻ ‘vaṭa’ eṉpataṟku ivvāṟu iraṇṭu arttam uḷḷattait tavaṟākappurintu koṇṭu rikvētam  (1, 24.7 atu ki.mu.1000 cārntatu ākalām) vāṉattil varuṇaṉ tāṅkum ālamaram oṉṟiṉ vērkaḷaippaṟṟik kūṟukiṟatu. harappā muttirai 179l ālamarattukkuḷ kāṇum maṉita urutteyvam inta marattaik kuṟippatākalām.

21. vētakkatai iṉṉoṉṟu, turuva naṭcattiramāṉatu ēṉai viṇmīṉkaḷōṭu kāṟṟuk kayiṟukaḷāl ropes of wind piṇaikkappaṭṭuḷḷatākak kūṟukiṟatu. inta aṭippaṭaiyil piṉvarum vācakaṅkaḷai parpōlā tarukiṟār.
hārappā H-9:  I	III= eḻumīṉ (captariṣi = Ursa mayor viṇmīṉ kūṭṭam

mokeñ 	M.414 vaṭamīṉ = turuva naṭcattiram

22. tirāviṭa muri (-v-, -nt -) [=to bend, to be bent] muruvu / muruku [=kammal, vaḷaiyal kaṭakam aḻaku] murukaṉ. eṉavē vaḷaiyattaic cintu eḻuttil iraṇṭu vaṭṭaṅkaḷ kuṟittaṉa. ēṉ iraṇṭu vaṭṭaṅkaḷ? oru vaṭṭam teḷivāṉa poruḷ tarātu; mēlum kammal, vaḷaiyal ākiyavaṟṟai iṇaiyākat tāṉē aṇikiṉṟaṉar? tamiḻnāṭṭilum ēṉ intiyā veṅkum (nalamāṉa kuḻantaip pēṟukkāka) cūluṟṟa tāykku vaḷaikāppu aṇital; puṉitamaraṅkaḷ, teyvaṅkaḷukku vaḷaiyal aṇivittal
ākiya caṭaṅkukaḷ uḷḷaṉa. ippiṉṉaṇiyil

mo 112 il kāṇum	III 	mo 241 il

aṟumīṉ (kiruttikā) 		= kāṇum vēḷ muruka

(murukaṉ)(kārttikēyaṉ) 		atē arttam

eṉṟu kūṟalām.

ninatōvāri muttirai Nd 1 l (mutukil aintuvari koṇṭa) aṇiṟpiḷḷai oṉṟaik kāṇalām. atē pōṉṟa aṇil marakkiḷaiyil talaikīḻākat toṅkik koṇṭiruppatai nauṣārō muttirai, mo 1202, hā 771 ākiyavaṟṟilum kāṇalām. avaṟṟil kāṇum iraṇṭaiyum murukappiḷḷai eṉappaṭikkalām. yāḻppāṉait tamiḻ āṭavar peyarkaḷil itupōṉṟu piḷḷai eṉṟu muṭiyum peyarkaḷ pala.

11 	eṉpatai murukavēḷ eṉappaṭittōm. atil II eṉpatu vēḷ tāṉ eṉa niṟuviṭa iṉṉoru cāṉṟaik kāṇalām:

vēḷ muruka 		mīṉ veḷ = veḷḷik kōḷ
(hā 723) 		hā 669

veḷ white;  ataṉuṭaiya oliyoppumaic col homophone tāṉ vēḷ. ivviraṇṭukkum iṉṉoru oliyoppumaic col veḷi (=open public, space (in general), intervening space [tēva rulakattukkum pūmikkum iṭaippaṭṭa (camaskiruta antarīkṣam), uḻavil irucālkaḷukkum iṭaippaṭṭa pakuti] eṉpatākum. vēṟu iṭaṅkaḷil varum III kōṭukaḷ kuṟiyīṭu mūvulakattaiyum kuṟippatākak karuta iṭamuṇṭu. tamiḻ “veḷḷi” veḷḷik kōḷai maṭṭumaṉṟi viṇ mīṉaiyum potuvākak kuṟikkiṟatu. (a.citamparanātaṉ ceṭṭiyārāṅkila - tamiḻ akarāti 1965: star = viṇmīṉ; vāṉveḷḷi) veḷḷiyai viṭiveḷḷi, viṭimīṉ eṉṟum aḻaippatuṇṭu.

23. ulakat tamiḻc cemmoḻi mānāṭṭu uraiyil (25-6-2010) parpōlā iṟutiyākak kūṟuvatu varumāṟu:

“oṉṟukkoṉṟu cariyākap poruntum rīpas muṟai vācippukaḷaik kaṇicamāṉa eṇṇikkaiyil kaṇṭōm. avaṟṟukku aṭittaḷamāka amaintu muḻu ātaravutarum moḻiyiyal ceytikaḷaiyum paṇpāṭṭuta taṉmaikaḷaiyum viḷakki immuṭivukaḷ taṟceyalāṉa pōli oppumaikaḷ allaveṉpataiyum niṟuviyuḷḷōm. aṟiviyal rītiyāṉa karāṟāṉa āyvu muṟaiyaip piṉpaṟṟi vācittuḷḷōm - māntaṉ moḻikaḷiṉ eḻuttu varivaṭivat (lipi) tōṟṟam vaḷarcci varalāṟu, puriyāta lipiyaip paṭittaṟiyak kaiyāḷa vēṇṭiya aṟivupūrvamāṉa muṟai, ivaṟṟaiyellām karuttiṟ koṇṭum, varalāṟṟu moḻiyiyaliṉ (tirāviṭa moḻikaḷiṉ oppu nōkku moḻiyiyal uṭpaṭa) āyvuc ceytikaḷiṉ piṉpulattilum ivvāyvu naṭantuḷḷatu. muttiraikaḷiṉ paṭa uruvaṅkaḷ etaik kuṟikkiṉṟaṉa veṉpataiyum mikak kavaṉamākavē karutip pārttuḷḷōm. paṇṭai intiyap paṇpāṭṭu varalāṟu harappā nākarikattiṉ (tōṟṟam, taṉmai, vaḷarcci, piṟa paṇpāṭukaḷuṭaṉ toṭarpu mutaliya) cūḻal ākiyavaṟṟai aṭiccaṭṭamākak koṇṭu pārttāl āyvil kūṟappaṭum muṭivukaḷ niyāyamāka ēṟkat takkavai eṉpatu teṟṟeṉa viḷaṅkum. ammuṭivukaḷum muḻumaiyāṉa teḷivāṉa cāṉṟukaḷum ātāraṅkaḷum amainta ciṟṟaḷaviṉavāṉa āyvuppulac ceytikaḷiṉ aṭippaṭaiyil tāṉ (maṟukkoṇāta vakaiyil) amaintuḷḷaṉa. [appulaṅkaḷ aracamarattōṭu iṇainta makappēṟu vēṇṭutal nampikkai; vāṉiyal, kālakkaṇippu ivaṟṟōṭu toṭarpuṭaiya mukkiyamāṉa paḻaiya intu matat toṉmakkatai; intumatattilum, paḻantamiḻ matattilum mutaṉmaipeṟṟa (murukaṉ pōṉṟa) teyvaṅkaḷ mutaliyaṉa]

mēṟcoṉṉavaṟṟiṉ aṭippaṭaiyil cintu eḻuttaip paṭittaṟivataṟkāṉa vāyil tiṟakkappaṭṭuviṭṭatu eṉa uṟutiyāka nāṉ nampukiṟēṉ. cintu muttirai vācakaṅkaḷ toltirāviṭa moḻiyiltāṉ eḻutappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa eṉpatait terintu koṇṭōm. anta lipiyiṉ amaippum evvaḻikaḷil muyaṉṟāl ataip paṭikkalām eṉpataiyum aṟintuḷḷōm. ituvarait tiṭṭavaṭṭamākat terintukoṇṭa ceytikaḷum avaṟṟiṉ virivāṉa cūḻalkaḷum mēṟkoṇṭu ceyya vēṇṭiya āyvukaḷukku vaḻikāṭṭukiṉṟaṉa, eṉiṉum cila cikkalkaḷ irukkattāṉ ceykiṉṟaṉa. pala paṭaveḻuttukaḷiṉ tiṭṭavaṭṭa māṉa poruḷ iṉṉum teriyavaravillai. toltirāviṭac coṟkaḷ, coṟṟoṭarkaḷ marapu vaḻakkukaḷ pōṉṟavaṟṟaip paṟṟiya ceytikaḷ namakku muḻumaiyākat teriyavillaiyākai yāl, poruttamāṉa vācippu muyaṟcikaḷ mūlam mēlum palavaṟṟaip paṭippataṟkum appaṭip paṭittu muṉ vaikkap paṭuvaṉavaṟṟai naṉku caripārttu aṟivulakam ēṟṟuk koḷḷum vakaiyil niṟuvuvataṟkum vāyppukaḷ mikakkuṟaivākavē uḷḷaṉa. inta nilaiyil tamiḻaiyum piṟa tirāviṭa moḻikaḷaiyum tāymoḻiyākap pēcupavarkaḷ inta āyvil ūkkattuṭaṉ talaippaṭṭu mēlum āyvu ceyya vēṇṭum. (vaṭa amerikkāvil mekcikō nāṭṭil uḷḷa) mayā nākarika eḻuttum col- acaiyaṉ (logo-syllabic) varivaṭivattiltāṉ uḷḷatu. mayā moḻiyait tāymoḻiyākak koṇṭavarkaḷukku anta lipiyaip paṭikkum muṟaikaḷil payiṟci aḷitta piṉṉar āyvu vēkamāka vaḷarcciyaṭaintatu. cintu muttirai varivaṭivattaip poṟuttum ōraḷavukku ituporuntum. palkalaikkaḻakap pērāciriyar pōṉṟa vallunar maṭṭumallātu. ārvamuḷḷa potumakkaḷum cintu muttiraippaṭa urukkaḷ etaik kuṟikkiṉṟaṉa eṉpatu kuṟittu cintittup poruttamāṉa karuttukaḷait terivikkalām; ivvāṟu ceyya oruvar tirāviṭa moḻi pēcunarākattāṉ irukka vēṇṭumeṉpatillai. eṉiṉum intiyap paṇpāṭu teṉāciya iyaṟkait tiṇaip poruḷkaḷ ākiyavaṟṟaip paṟṟiya tiṟamāṉa pulamai iruntāl ittakaiya āyvu mēlum ciṟakkum tamiḻ makkaḷ ivvakaiyil terivikkum āyvukkaruttukaḷaiyellām ceṉṉaiyil (taramaṇi maiya pāliṭekṉik vaḷākam) rōjā muttaiyā āyvu nūlakattil airāvatam makātēvaṉ niṟuviyuḷḷa cintu āyvu maiyam oruṅkiṇaittu ūkkuvikkalām. veḷippaṭum taramāṉa karuttukaḷai ammaiyam taṉatu valaittaḷattilum veḷiyiṭalām.

24. ittuṟaiyil mēlum muṉṉēṟṟam kāṇa vēṇṭumāyiṉ paṇpāṭṭu āyvu vallunar, tamiḻiya moḻiyiyal vallunar, akaḻvāyvu - paṇṭai nākarika āyvāḷar ākiyōruṭaiya oruṅkiṇainta uḻaippu tēvai eṉṟa āṇṭru rāpiṉcaṉ 2001 karuttum ituvē: Lost languages: the enigma of the world”s undeciphered scripts McGraw Hill; New york (“a reanalysis of the seals and other inscriptions on the basis of their chronological and spatial distribution .... if combined with a more intensive analysis of the signs and their patterns, the inputs of “cultural’ experts like Parpola and Mahadevan, and the linguistic control provided by professional Dravidianists may in due course yield solid results. Collaboration seems to be the name of the game here, since no one scholar can hope to command sufficient knowledge of ancient writing systems, Indus valley and the relevant languages.

ai. makātēvaṉ

25. parpōlāvai aṭuttu cintu eḻuttu āyvil nāṟpatu aimpatu āṇṭukaḷāka āyvu neṟi piṟaḻāmal muṉaippuṭaṉ īṭupaṭṭuḷḷa aṟiñar ai. makātēvaṉ i.ā.pa. ēḻāṇṭu kaṭumaiyāka uḻaittu 1977l veḷiyiṭṭa cintu muttirai vācakaṅkaḷiṉ niralpaṭuttiya aṭaṅkaṟpaṭṭiyalai piṟṟai āyvāḷar aṉaivarum payaṉpaṭutti varukiṉṟaṉar. irumoḻi vācakam / coṟpaṭṭiyalkoṇṭa poṟippu etuvum kiṭaittā
loḻiya cintu muttirai vācakaṅkaḷai muḻumaiyākappaṭittaṟiya iyalātu eṉṉum (parpōlā mutaliya) ittuṟai vallunar palaruṭaiya karuttē ivaruṭaiyatumām.. cintup poṟippukaḷ “oru collukku oru eḻuttu” “oru acaikku oru eḻuttu”ākiya iruvakai varivaṭivamum kalantatāka ivar karutukiṟār. ammuttirai vācakaṅkaḷiṉ moḻi paḻantirāviṭam eṉṟa parpōlā mutaliya nallaṟiñar karuttē makātēvaṉ karuttumākum. piṉvarum aṭippaṭaikaḷiṉmēl - atāvatu :-

(a) oru collukku oru eḻuttu āka amaiyum cilavaṟṟait teṉṉintiyāvil kiṭṭiyuḷḷa cintu eḻuttukaḷiṉ eccaṅkaḷ cā [ki.mu. mutal nūṟṟāṇṭu cārnta cūlūr taṭṭu; (tiṇṭivaṉam arukil uḷḷa) cāṇūr īmattāḻip poṟippukaḷ; kēraḷa mānilam paṭṭaṇattil (muciṟi?) kiṭṭiya ōṭṭut tuṇṭil uḷḷa muruku kuṟiyīṭu; ki.mu.1500 cārnta cempiyaṉkaṇṭiyūr kaṟkōṭariyil uḷḷa (makātēvaṉ “muruku = aṉ - eṉappaṭittuḷḷa) poṟippu; pōṉṟavaṟṟiṉ aṭippaṭaiyilum;

(ā) cintuppakuti makkaḷukkum, aṅku ciṟu eṇṇikkaiyil intō āriya moḻi pēcunarukkum ki.mu.2500-1500 kāla aḷavil nikaḻnta palatuṟaik kalappup paṇpāṭṭu eccaṅkaḷ vētamoḻi / camaskiruta nūlkaḷ; caṅka ilakkiyaṅkaḷ; piṟavaralāṟṟuc cāṉṟukaḷ ākiyavaṟṟilkāṇappaṭuvatāl avaṟṟaiyum nuṇuki ārāynta aṭippaṭaiyilum;

makātēvaṉ cintu muttirai vācakaṅkaḷ kāṭṭum paṇpāṭu tamiḻakap paṇpāṭṭai ottiruntatu eṉṟa karutukōḷukku vantu atukuṟittu tamiḻnāṭu varalāṟṟup pēravaiyiṉ 16vatu karuttaraṅkil (9-10-2009; tirucci) Vestiges of Indus Civilization in old Tamil eṉṟa āyvuraiyai veḷiyiṭṭār. 2010 caṉavariyil avvariya āyvuraiyait tamiḻākki “cintuveḷip paṇpāṭum caṅka ilakkiyamum” eṉṟa peyaril cemmoḻit tamiḻāyvu mattiya niṟuvaṉam veḷiyiṭṭuḷḷatu.

26. mēṟcoṉṉa 9-10-09 āyviliruntum itē aṭippaṭaiyil makātēvaṉ veḷiyiṭṭuḷḷa vēṟucila āyvuk kaṭṭuraikaḷil iruntum cila ceytikaḷait taruvatu porutta mākum:
 
(i) muruku (murukaṉ) eṉa aṭaiyāḷam kaṇṭuḷḷa cintu ṉainaṭipacayaa karuttukkuṟiyīṭu cempiyaṉ kaṇṭiyūrkaṟ kōṭariyil uḷḷatu.

(ii) poṟu = kāvu (puṟam 206 il varum ‘kāviṉemkalaṉē’ eṉpatil kāvutal cumattal cumattal eṉṉum poruḷil vantatu) ->poṟai -> rikvēta parata Bharata  (oru mukkiyamāṉa kuṭiyiṉar). kāvaṭiyaic cuṭṭiya cintu vaṭivam piṉvarumāṟu ciṟu māṟṟaṅkaḷōṭu vēta camaskirutac coṟkaḷaik kuṟittaṉa eṉalām.

āka peru vīraṉ eṉṉum poruḷ koṇṭa

vaṭivattai makātēvaṉ eṉṟa poruḷ koṇṭa camaskirutac col vaṭivamākak koṇṭaṉar. ammoḻiyil ḷakarattai lakaramākak koṇṭu ‘nīlakaṇṭaṉ’ eṉattavaṟākak karutip pāṟkaṭalaik kaṭaintu uruvāṉa “(nīla niṟa)nañcai uṇṭa kaḻuttai uṭaiyavaṉ” eṉap purāṇakkataiyum piṉṉarp puṉaiyappaṭṭu makātēvaṉ nīlakaṇṭaṉ āṉāṉ. (nīḷ kaṇṭaṉ eṉṟa toltamiḻp peyarai maṟantuviṭṭa nilaiyil) kaṟai / nīla maṇi miṭaṟṟu aṇṇal eṉṟa putiya peyarai vaṭamoḻi “nīla kaṇṭaṉiṉ’ coṟpeyarppākakkoṇṭu, appurāṇak kataiyaiyum ēṟkumnilai puṟanāṉūṟṟuk kālattilēyē ēṟpaṭṭuviṭṭatu. teṉṉāṭṭil vaḻaṅkum nīlakaṇṭaṉ, maṇikaṇṭaṉ, nañcuṇṭaṉ mutaliya peyarkaḷiṉ uṇmaiyāṉa varalāṟu ituvē.


irā. mativāṇaṉ

27. tamiḻiya (tirāviṭa) moḻi pēcunar ki.mu.10000 kku muṉṉarē intiyāveṅkum paraviyiruntaṉar eṉpataiyum (tirāviṭar intiyāvai viṭṭu vaṭakkē paraviya) tirāviṭar ēṟṟattiṉ Dravidian Ascent  piṉṉar intō - airōppiyam, urāl
- altāyk mutaliya moḻik kuṭumpaṅkaḷ atiliruntu uruvākiyirukkalām eṉpataiyum mutal iyalil kaṇṭōm. ñā.tēvanēyaṉ tamiḻvaralāṟu (1967) il kūṟiyatu varumāṟu.
 
“mokañcotarō muttiraiyeḻuttukkaḷ tamiḻeḻuttuk kaḷiṉ mūla vaṭivaik kāṭṭukiṉṟaṉaveṉiṉ, atu avai tamiḻeḻuḻuttukkaḷ vaḷarcciyaṭaiyāta nilaiyil vaṭakkē ceṉṟa tamiḻar kaiyāṇṭa eḻuttu muṟai eṉpataiyallātu tamiḻar vaṭakkiṉiṉṟu teṟkē vantār eṉṟu uṇarttātu” 

28. tamiḻiṉ ilakkaṇak kaṭṭamaippai aṭippaṭaiyākak koṇṭum, tamiḻc coṟpiṟappu neṟi muṟaikaḷaip poruttiyum cintu eḻuttukkaḷai 1990 ai oṭṭip paṭikkat toṭaṅkiya irā. mativāṇaṉ muttiraikaḷaip patittāl kiṭaikkum vācakaṅkaḷai iṭamiruntu valamākap L-> R  paṭittuḷḷār. Indus script Dravidian (1995) eṉṉum nūlil cintu veḷi nākarika muttiraic coṟkaḷ 3000ttaiyum perumpālāṉa toltamiḻ iyaṟ peyarkaḷākap paṭittuḷḷār. vaṇikar, talaivar, kaṭavuḷar pōṉṟavarkaḷiṉ peyarkaḷai acaiyeḻuttukkaḷil (Morpho - syllabic) im muttiraikaḷil poṟittuḷḷaṉar eṉpatum,

cāttaṉ, mācāttaṉ, cāṉaṉ, vaṅkaṉ, nattattaṉ, nantaṉ, ōmaṉ, cāmaṉ, evvi, aṉaṅkaṉ, oḷiyaṉ, cēyaṉ, atiyaṉ, avvaṉ, ayyaṉ, kō avvaṉ, yāṉaṉ, paṉṉaṉ, avuṇaṉ, kāṭṭaṉ

pōṉṟa peyarkaḷ muttiraikaḷiṟ paravalāka varukiṉṟaṉa eṉpatum mativāṇaṉ kaṇṭu piṭippu ākum. ivar kuṟi       ai ‘ka’ eṉṟum kuṟi       ai ‘ca’ eṉṟum paṭittuḷḷār. cila muttirai vācakaṅkaḷ piṉṉar tarappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.     eṉṉum cuvattikam cintuveḷi eḻuttil “ōm” (ōmpuka -ōm) eṉpatu ivar kaṇṭupiṭippu. pacupati muttiraiyai ivar ‘kā cā kō avvaṉ” eṉṟu paṭikkiṟār.

pukāril ēṟṟumati, iṟakkumatiyāṉa poruḷkaḷ mītu cōḻaṉiṉ puli muttirai poṟikkappaṭṭataip paṭṭiṉappālai “puli poṟittup puṟam pōkki” eṉak kuṟikkiṟatu. apporuḷ kaḷiṉ mēl vaṇikarkaḷiṉ peyar muttiraikaḷ poṟikkap paṭṭatai, “vampa mākkaḷ tampeyar poṟittuk kaṇṇeḻuttup paṭutta eṇṇup pal poti”eṉa cilappatikāram (5:111-112) kuṟikkiṟatu. icceytikaḷ cintuveḷi eḻuttu vācakaṅkaḷai iyaṟpeyarāka mativāṇaṉ paṭittatai araṇ ceykiṉṟaṉa.




pūrṇa cantira jīvā

avaruṭaiya (2004) “cintuveḷiyil muntu tamiḻ” 
nūl maṟṟum piṟṟai āyvuraikaḷil kāṇum muṭivukaḷ.

29. jīvāviṉ muṭivukaḷiṉ curukkam varumāṟu:

1) cintu eḻuttu varivaṭivaṅkaḷ acainilai syllabic  vakai cārntavai. (antacintuvarivaṭivaṅkaḷiliruntupiṉṉar uruvāṉa) caṅka kālat tamiḻi (tamiḻ - pirāmi) varivaṭivattiṟku avaiyē mūlamākum.

ii) piṉṉar cintu varivaṭivaṅkaḷiliruntē oli nilai vaṭivam phonetic script  karuttu uruvākiyatu. cintu makkaḷiṭamiruntu ik karuttaik kaṭaṉvāṅkiyē (ulakiṉ mutal neṭuṅkaṇakku varicai, alphabet eṉap potuvākak karutappaṭum) piṉīciya eḻuttu uruvākkappaṭṭatu. ki.mu.1500 ai oṭṭiya piṉīciya varivaṭiviliruntu pirāmiyō tamiḻiyō, uruvāyiṉaveṉpatu piḻai.

iii) nāṉūṟṟukku mēṟpaṭṭa cintu varivaṭivaṅkaḷil oru cilavē collacaiyaṉ logosyllabic  vaṭivukaḷ. ēṉaiyavai iṭu kuṟivaṭivaṅkaḷ. attakaiya iṭukuṟi vavaṅkaḷ 45. avaṟṟuḷ pala kūṭṭeḻuttukkaḷāka eḻutappaṭṭaṉa. (tamiḻ moḻiyiṉ collamaippu , puṇarcci pōṉṟavaṟṟiṉ aṭippaṭaiyil ikkūṭṭeḻuttukaḷ uruvākkappaṭṭiruntaṉa. āḻnta tamiḻaṟivu uṭaiyavar maṭṭumē ikkūṭṭeḻuttukaḷil aṭaṅkiya kuṟiyīṭukaḷaip pirittaṟiya iyalum) cintueḻuttil 18 meyyeḻuttukkaḷukku 16 vaṭivaṅkaḷ iruntaṉa. mīṉvaṭivam la,ḻa,ḷa mūṉṟukkum potuvāṉatu. uyirolikaḷukku 6 vaṭivaṅkaḷ iruntaṉa. ākamottam 22 vaṭivaṅkaḷ.

iv) cintu veḷi nakara nākarikam cumār ki.mu.1500l aḻintu viṭṭa pōtilumkirāmappuṟaṅkaḷil cintu eḻuttuk kuṟiyīṭukaḷ toṭarntu puḻakkattil iruntirukka vēṇṭum. kuṟippāka teṉṉāṭṭup peruṅkaṟkāla Megalithic (ki.mu.1400 - 800) kuṟiyīṭukaḷāka avai payaṉpaṭuttappaṭṭu vantuḷḷaṉa (kīḻavālai; perumukkal). (2006 pipravariyil cempiyaṉ kaṇṭiyūr kaṟkaruviyil (ki.mu. 1000-kku muntiyatu) uḷḷa cintu eḻuttukaḷ samakātēvaṉ karuttuppaṭi muru(ku) - aṉ - ? - ?”] cīvā karuttai meyppikkiṉṟaṉa.

30. tamiḻi eḻuttiṉ tōṟṟattai cintuveḷi nākarika eḻuttōṭu toṭarpu paṭuttiyuḷḷatu maṭṭumiṉṟi paṇṭait tamiḻaka varalāṟṟuk kūṟukaḷ, tamiḻakat tolliyal eccaṅkaḷ pōṉvaṟṟaiyum annākarikattōṭu toṭarpu paṭuttip pala ariya ceytikaḷait tantuḷḷār jīvā. eṉavē cintu nākarikam, eḻuttu paṟṟi ārvam koṇṭōr maṭṭumaṉṟi tamiḻ, tamiḻar, tamiḻaka varalāṟṟu ārvalarkaḷum innūlaip paṭittāl avarkaḷukkup pala ariya ceytikaḷum karuttukaḷum kiṭaikkum.

31. parpōlā uḷḷiṭṭa mēlai aṟiñar palarum cintu eḻuttāṉatu paṭavurukkaḷuṭaṉ toṭarpuṭaiya collacaiyaṉ (Logo Syllabic) eṉṟa tavaṟāṉa uṉṉippil āyvu ceytu varukiṉṟaṉar eṉpar jīvā. cintu eḻuttu uṇmaiyil kūṭṭeḻuttu vaṭivaṅkaḷaiyē koṇṭatu eṉiṉum ataṉ aḻakiya ōviya - paṭa uruttaṉmai yoṉṟaiyē kaṇṭu, avarkaḷ collacaiyaṉ ākakkaruti iḻukkiyuḷḷaṉar eṉpatai avar piṉvarumāṟu viḷakkukiṟār.

(i) ulaka nākarikaṅkaḷiṉ tolleḻuttukaḷ aṉaittum paṭikkappaṭṭuviṭṭaṉa. paṭavuruttaṉmai koṇṭa cumēriya āppeḻuttu (Cuneiform), ekiptiya puṉitappoṟippu (Hieroglyphs) ‘paṭavurut taṉmai’ aṭippaṭaiyil paṭikkap peṟṟuviṭṭaṉa. uyirmeyyaṉ (Syllabary) eḻuttu muṟaiyāṉa līṉiyar - B maṟṟum caipras eḻuttukaḷum oppīṭṭu muṟaiyil paṭikkappeṟṟuviṭṭaṉa. cintu eḻuttum “muṟṟilum paṭavuruvaṉ muṟaikkuriya collacaiyaṉ eḻuttu muṟaiyāka” iruntiruppiṉ itaṟkuḷ paṭittiruppar.

(ii) cintu eḻuttiṉaip paṭikka marapumuṟaip pārvaiyai viṭuttu (māṟṟuccintaṉai aṭippaṭaiyil) evaṟṟuṭaṉum cārāta, nuṇṇāyvu tēvaippaṭukiṟatu. ataṉ vaṭivam enta oru iyaṟkaip poruḷiṉ aṭippaṭaiyaiyum koṇṭataṉṟu. avvāṟu irukakalāmeṉa mēmpōkkākak karutavaikkum oṉṟiraṇṭum paṭattukkuriya poruḷaic cuṭṭuvatākak karutamuṭiyavillai. nuṇuki ārāyntāl cintu eḻuttai vaṭivamaippu aṭippaṭaiyil (1) eḷiya aṭippaṭai vaṭivaṅkaḷ, (2) kuṟiyīṭēṟṟa aṭippaṭai vaṭivaṅkaḷ, maṟṟum (3) kūṭṭu vaṭivaṅkaḷ eṉṟa mūṉṟāka pakukkalām. (ivaṟṟait tavira toṭarnilaiyil ēṟpaṭum iṭaiveḷiyai nirappa paṭavurukkaḷ, kuṟiyīṭukaḷ, eṇkaḷ maṟṟum cintu eḻuttumuṟaikku muntiya eḻuttu muṟaikaḷiṉ ecca vaṭivaṅkaḷaiyumkalantu ōr oruṅkiṇainta eḻuttu muṟaiyai amaittuḷḷaṉar.) ekiptiya eḻuttu muṟai ittakaiyateṉṟu sveyṉ mutaliyavarkaḷ kūṟukiṉṟaṉar.

(iii) immūvakai vaṭivaṅkaḷukkum aṭippaṭaiyāṉa ceyaṟkaiyāṉa vaṭiva iyal (Geometric) taṉmaiyuṭaiya aṭippaṭai vaṭivaṅkaḷ varumāṟu



ivaṟṟai ōviyat taṉmai koṇṭa paṭavurukkaḷāka evarum karuta iyalātu. iv vaṭivaṅkaḷaippaṟṟi nuṇuki ārāyvatē mutaṟpaṭiyākum.

mēṟkaṇṭa vaṭivaṅkaḷuṭaṉ mēlum cila eḷiyaṉavum kuṟiyīṭu ēṟkātaṉavumāṉa aṭippaṭai vaṭivaṅkaḷum cintu eḻuttuppaṭṭiyalil uḷḷaṉa. ai. makātēvaṉ (1977) cintu muttirait tokuppu nūl aṭippaṭaiyil avaṟṟaiyum tokuttu intap paṭṭiyalaik kīḻkkaṇṭavāṟu niṟaivu ceyyalām.

(ivai mūṉṟuvakai vaṭivaṅkaḷiliruntum piritteṭuttavai, māṟṟu vaṭivaṅkaḷaiyum, oppuṭai vaṭivaṅkaḷaiyum vilakkit tokuttavai; ivaṟṟait tavira vēṟu vaṭivam etaiyum cintu eḻuttil kāṭṭaiyalātu eṉpatu uṟuti.

icceyaṟkai vaṭivaṅkaḷiṉ uruvākkam evvāṟu nikaḻntatu? potuvāka nākarika vaḷarcciyōṭu moḻivaḷarcciyum, nakara nākarika vaḷarcciyōṭu eḻuttu varivaṭiva muṟai vaḷarcciyum piṇaintatu ākum. cintu nākarika nakara vaḷarcci nilaikku oppāṉa vaḷarcciyai ataṉ varivaṭiva muṟaiyilum kāṇkiṟōm. nakaramaippu, kaṭṭakkalai, aḷavai muṟaikaḷ, toḻilnuṭpam eṉṟu palvēṟu tuṟaikaḷiṉ vaḷarcciyiṉ mūlam peṟṟa aṉupava aṟivaieḻuttu varivaṭiva amaippilum payaṉpaṭuttiyuḷḷaṉar eṉalām. akamatu hacaṉ tāṉi (Dani) 1963 / II. 1985 nūlākiya Indian Palelography - iṉ 19m pakkamum jīvā karuttai ātarikkiṟatu. kuṟiyīṭu ēṟkāta aṭippaṭaivaṭivaṅkaḷ 54 tāṉ eṉpar aṟiñar tāṉi.

32. cintu eḻuttiṉ mikuvaḷarcciyum paravalum iruvakai yāṉatu eṉpar jīvā. muttirai eḻuttiṉ paraval makārāṭṭira taymāpāttuṭaṉ (Daimabad) muṭikiṟatu. eḻuttut toṭarpāṉa paraval, ilaṅkai yāḻppāṇamvaraic ceṉṟatu. kujarāttil uḷḷa pēṭtuvārakai, tamiḻakattil potikai malaikkukai, kīḻvālai, perumukkal mutaliyaṉa; ilaṅkaiyilyāḻppāṇam iṅkellām inta eḻuttu muṟaiyaiyum, ataṉ toṭarvaḷarcci nilaiyaiyum kāṇalām.

(i) kujarāt pēṭtuvārakai :

vvr ṇā ṇa vara y ṇ ma
= maṇyvaraṇayāvvōr

(valamiruntu) vaṭivaṅkaḷ 1, 3 cintu eḻuttu tamiḻiyākum māṟṟattaik kāṭṭukiṉṟaṉa.

(ii) perumukkal (tamiḻnāṭu) :

a ra c r = aracar

eḻuttukaḷ 2, 3, 4 il tamiḻi eḻuttākum māṟṟam uḷḷatu.

(iii) kīḻvālai (tamiḻnāṭu) :

tava cayya carai = 	tavacayya carai

ivvaṭivaṅkaḷ vaṭṭāra vaḻakku vaṭivaṅkaḷāka uḷḷaṉa.

(iv) potikai malaikkukai (tamiḻnāṭu) intiyaṉ ekspiras 23.3.1985

= mū ay tavaṇantaṉ iṉ tava

ivveḻut tukkaḷ kālattāl muntiyavaiyum cintu eḻuttai naṉkaṟintavar

(v) yāḻppāṇam āṉaikkoṭṭaṭi (ilaṅkai) The Hindu  26.4.1981 intirapālā kaṭṭurai)

ma ya ṉa = mayaṉa
t vē kō = kōvētta

itu oru kuyavar muttirai. kōvēttu - vēṭkōvar = kuyavar

mēṟkaṇṭa aintum teḷivāṉa cintu eḻuttu vaṭivaṅkaḷākavum, tamiḻiyāka māṟum teḷivāṉa māṟṟaṅkaḷuṭaṉum uḷḷaṉa. tamiḻiyākum māṟṟattil pēṭtuvārakai toṭakka nilaiyaiyum, perumukkal iṭainilaiyaiyum, yāḻppāṇam muḻumaiyākat tamiḻiyākiviṭṭa iṟuti nilaiyaiyum kāṭṭukiṉṟaṉa. ataṉpiṉṉar, tamiḻiyiṉ muḻuvaḷarcci ki.mu.800 - 600 kāla aḷavil nikaḻntirukka vēṇṭum.

33. cintu eḻuttu tamiḻiyākum māṟṟam tamiḻakattiltāṉ naṭaipeṟṟuḷḷatu. tamiḻakattup paṇṭaip pāṉaiyōṭukaḷil kūṭa cintu eḻuttu vaṭivaṅkaḷ iruppatiliruntu itaṉai aṟiyalām. tamiḻakap peruṅkaṟkāla vāḻiṭaṅkaḷākiya koṭumaṇal, cāṇūr pōṉṟavaṟṟil kiṭaittuḷḷa ivviruvakai eḻuttu vaṭivaṅkaḷ veṟumpāṉaikkīṟalkaḷāka maṭṭum irukka muṭiyātu e-ṭu.
cāṇūr (ki.mu.1400-800: es.ār. rāv):

cāṇūr 1400-800 BC  		L < - R
ā ya var = 		āyavar.

34. cintu eḻuttum tamiḻi eḻuttum oṉṟu kalanta nilaiyil kiṭaippatu avaṟṟiṉ uṟavu - marapu - varalāṟṟut toṭarpait teḷivākkukiṟatu. ivaṟṟiṉ aṭippaṭai vaṭivaṅkaḷiṭaiyē vaṭiva - olinilai oppumaikaḷ mikap porunti varukiṉṟaṉa. (oṉṟiraṇṭu vaṭivaṅkaḷ maṭṭumē caṟṟu māṟupaṭṭavai) e-ṭu.



cintu ṅa,na,ṉa meykaḷ tamiḻiyil māṟiyuḷḷaṉa. mīṉ vaṭivuṭaṉ ḻa maṭṭumē poruntukiṟatu. la-ḷa vaṭivaṅkaḷuṭaṉ cintu eḻuttētum poruntavillai. eṉavē, ivai piṟkālattil uruvākkappeṟṟirukka vēṇṭum. tamiḻiyiṉ moḻiyākka aṭippaṭaiyil pārttāl cintu eḻuttil mīṉ vaṭivam la-ḻa-ḷa mūṉṟaiyum olikkap payaṉpaṭṭirukka vēṇṭum.

ay, av vaṭivaṅkaḷ cintuvil akara eḻuttaṭippaṭaiyil oṉṟu/iraṇṭu kōṭukaḷiṭṭu amaikkap peṟṟuḷḷaṉa. avvolikaḷ toṭar nilaiyil avvāṟē ay, av eṉa olippatāl appaṭiyē iṅku payaṉpaṭukiṟatu.

(ii) cintu eḻuttuk kūṭṭu vaṭivaṅkaḷum, cintu aṭippaṭai vaṭivaṅkaḷaik koṇṭu amaikkappeṟṟavaiyē. ākaiyāl avaṟṟaip pirittaṟintu, tamiḻiyuṭaṉ piṉvarumāṟu porutti yuḷḷār jīvā:

ikkūṭṭu vaṭivaṅkaḷ cintu - tamiḻi eḻuttumuṟaikaḷiṉ vaṭiva - olinilai oṟṟumaiyaik kāṭṭukiṉṟaṉa.

cintu - tamiḻi eḻuttukaḷiṭaiyē uḷḷa vaṭiva oppumai - oli oppumai aṭippaṭaiyil cintu muttiraip poṟippukaḷiṉ 1977 makātēvaṉ tokuppil āṅkāṅku mātirikaḷait tiraṭṭi cilavācakaṅkaḷai tamiḻiyākap paṭittuk kāṭṭiyuḷḷār jīvā (tamatu 2010 cūṉ ulakattamiḻc cemmoḻi mānāṭṭu āyvuk kaṭṭuraiyil). ovvoṉṟilum iraṇṭāvatu vari “tamiḻi” ākum.

35. cintu eḻuttu meyyeḻuttai mikutiyākak koṇṭa ceyaṟkai uyirmeyyākiya - acaiyeḻuttu muṟaiyeṉṟu uṟutiyākiṟatu. cintu eḻuttu nilaviya kālattilēyē, ki.mu.2250 aḷavil kirīṭ tīvil miṉōvaṉ araciṉ līṉiyar-B  uyirmeyyaṉ (Syllabary) eḻuttumuṟai vaḻaṅkiyatu. uyirmeyyaṉ eḻuttē olinilai eḻuttiṉ toṭakkamākum. itiliruntē meyyeḻuttu muṟaikaḷ tōṉṟiṉa. cintu eḻuttu muṟaiyilum paḻaiya uyirmeyyaṉ eḻuttu muṟaikkuriya cāṉṟeccaṅkaḷ uḷḷaṉa. uyirmeyyaṉ muṟaiyiṉ payaṉpāṭṭiluḷḷa kaṭuñ cumaiyāl makkaḷ meyyeḻuttu varivaṭivaṅkaḷaik uruvākkip payaṉpaṭuttiṉar. cintu makkaḷum ki.mu.2500 aḷavil ataṉaiyē ceytirukka vēṇṭum. meyyeḻuttu muṟaiyē olinilai (Phonetic) eḻuttiṉ tāyākaiyāl, cintu eḻuttē ulaka aḷavil olinilai eḻuttiṉ tāyeṉalām.

cintu eḻuttaip paṭittaṟiya utavum “rōcaṭṭā poṟippu” ākat tamiḻik kalveṭṭu eḻuttukaḷaik koṇṭu, mēṟkaṇṭavāṟu cintup poṟippukaḷait tamiḻi eḻuttukaḷiṉ aṭippaṭaiyil paṭittu iraṇṭuvakai eḻuttukaḷum vaṭivanilai - olinilaikaḷil poruntiyuḷḷaṉa eṉṟu jīvā koṇṭuḷḷār.

36. jīvā āyviṉpaṭi cintu, tamiḻi, tamiḻ eḻuttu varivaṭiva oppumaippaṭṭiyal varumāṟu (82%  aḷavukku oppumai uḷḷatu. cintu eḻuttukaḷ nēr kōṭṭu vaṭivaṅkaḷ; āṉāl paṉai olaiyil eḻutap perumaḷavukku potumakkaḷ payaṉpaṭuttiya tāla tamiḻi vaṭivaṅkaḷ vaḷainta urukkaḷai aṭaintaṉa)




37. “tamiḻi” acaiyeḻuttu (syllabic) varivaṭivamtāṉ; eṉiṉum meyyeḻuttu vaṭivaṅkaḷil olimāṟṟak kuṟikaḷaic diacritic marks cērttu tamiḻi uruvākiyatu. ki.mu.800kkup piṉṉar “teṉṉāṭṭup peruṅkaṟkālat tamiḻa nākarittiṉariṭam tamiḻi mēlum māṟṟam aṭaintatu. cintu eḻuttukaḷiṭamiruntu nēraṭiyāka olinilai vaṭivai phonetic concept, piṉicīyar kaṭaṉ vāṅki piṉīciya varivaṭivai amaittuk koṇṭaṉar; āṉāl olimāṟṟak kuṟikaḷaip payaṉpaṭuttāmal “mey + uyir = uyirmey” eṉṟavāṟu ‘acaiyeḻuttai syllabary amaittuk koṇṭaṉar. cintu mey eḻuttu varivaṭivaṅkaḷ cilavaṟṟaiyum avaṟkaḷ tamatu piṉīciya varivaṭivattil ēṟṟup payaṉpaṭuttiyuḷḷaṉar. ivaiyellām jīvāviṉ āyvu muṭivukaḷ.

cintu muttiraikaḷaip patittapiṉ kiṭaikkum vācakaṅkaḷai eppaṭip paṭikka vēṇṭum? (perumpālōr paṭippatu valamiruntu iṭamāka (L <- R)

38. muttiraikaḷaip patittapiṉ kiṭṭum vācakaṅkaḷai valamiruntu iṭamākap paṭippōrē perumpāṉmaiyarāvar (kāṭ Gadd,  smit, mārṣal, haṇṭar, hīrās, parpōlā, cālek, makātēvaṉ, jīvā uṭpaṭa), avarkaḷ tarum kāraṇaṅkaḷ curukkamāka:

(1) kalipaṅkaṉ ōṭṭup poṟippil (kāṇum) eṉpatil tāṉ iṟuti eḻuttu, (lāl : 1966) cintu muttiraip poṟipput toṭarkaḷil U iṟuti eḻuttu; / ivviraṇṭum toṭakka eḻuttukaḷ.

(2) muttirai vācakaṅkaḷil uḷḷa vilaṅkukaḷ perumpālum iṭappakkam nōkkiyuḷḷaṉa.

(3) (a) hārappā 103 vācakattaip piṉvarumāṟu paṭikka vēṇṭum:
 
(ā) moheñcotarō 735 vācakattil kaṭaicik kuṟi iṭappakkattil kaṭaiciyāka iṭukkil varaiyappaṭṭuḷḷatu.



mo 66


iṇai eṅkum kiṭṭavillai

varicai 76 taṭavai varukiṟatu.

(4) iṭamiruntu valamākap paṭippatē cari eṉpataṟku rāmaṉ (1986); mativāṇaṉ (2002) ākiyōr tarum kāraṇaṅkaḷ:-

i) kalipaṅkaṉ pāṉai ōṭṭuc cil muḻumaiyaṟṟatu; pāṉaiyil eppaṭiyiruntatu, vācakattil entappakuti intac cillil vantatu eṉpatu teriyātu.

ii) ki.mu.1400 varai ulakil enta lipiyum L <- R  āka eḻutappaṭavillai. ataṟkuppiṉṉarum, vaṭa cemittik maṭṭumē appaṭi eḻutappaṭṭatu. ki.mu.2600 lēyēuruvāṉa cintulipi akkālattiya piṟa poṟippukaḷaip pōlavē L -> R ākappaṭikka vēṇṭiyatakat tāṉ iruntirukkam. tiṭumeṉa evarum muṉṉataip piṉṉatāka māṟṟiyirār.

iii) iṉṟum L  <-  R āka uḷḷa mikaccila moḻikaḷākiya arapu mutaliyavaṟṟilum eṇkaḷ (avai L  <-  R  vaḻaṅkum intiyā viṭamiruntu peṟṟavai) L -> R ākavē eḻutapṭukiṉṟaṉa.

(iv) cintumoḻi tamiḻiya moḻi eṉṟu ēṟattāḻa muṭivāṉa nilaiyil tamiḻ ilakkaṇa moḻiyiyal aṭippaṭaiyil (L -> R) uṭpaṭa) paṭippatē aṟivuṭaimaiyākum. appaṭippaṭittu “aṉaittuc cintup poṟippukaḷaiyum tām poruttamāka vācittu viṭṭatē L -> Rkku uṟutiyāṉa cāṉṟu” eṉpar mativāṇaṉ. ki.mu.1500kkup piṟpaṭṭa teṉṉintiyāvil kāṇum cintu lipikaḷ uḷḷiṭṭa / cārnta poṟippukaḷilum L -> R ākavē uḷḷaṉa eṉpatu maṟṟoru kāraṇam eṉpar.

(v) irāmaṉuṭaiya 1986 kaṭṭuraiyil muttirai vācakaṅkaḷil tām kāṇum taṉmaikaḷ (muttiraikaḷaip patittuk kiṭaikkum vācakaṅkaḷil alla) atāvatu:

iṭappakka iṟutiyil kuṟiyīṭukaḷ ciṟiyatāka iruppatu, allatu iṭittuk koṇṭiruppatu. (colliṉ mutal eḻuttai muttiraiyiṉ valappakkattiliruntu poṟikkat toṭaṅki muṭikkum nilaiyil iṭappakkattil iṭakkuṟaivu ēṟpaṭṭatāl innilaimai; iṭappakka iṟutiyil mārjiṉ illātatu; iṭappakka iṟutiyil ōriru eḻuttukaḷ oṉṟaṉ kīḻ oṉṟāka varutal; mutaliyavai

patippitta vācakaṅkaḷ L -> R āka irukkum vakaiyil muttiraikaḷil L -> R ākac cetukkappaṭṭatai niṟuvukiṉṟaṉa eṉṟa vātattaik kāṇalām.

cintu eḻuttu vari vaṭivattukkum piṟṟait tamiḻi
vari vaṭivattukkum uḷḷa toṭarpu

39. cintu varivaṭivattiliruntu tamiḻi (pirami) varivaṭivu uruvākiyatu eṉṟu karutuvatu tavaṟu eṉṟa parpōlā karuttai mēlē kuṟittōm. māṟāka avvāṟu tāṉ uruvākiyatu eṉpataṟkāṉa piṟa aṟiñar cilar karuttukaḷai ippirivil kāṇpōm.

40. mikap paḻaiya caṅka nūlkaḷililēyē (tolkāppiyam, eṭṭuttokai nūlkaḷil aṭaṅkiya pāṭalkaḷil toṉmai cāṉṟavai) tamiḻ varivaṭivam, eḻuttumuṟai kuṟittum paṇpaṭṭa ilakkiyam kuṟittum teḷivāka, virivākak kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷatu. ai. makātēvaṉ (2003)karuttu, “acōkaṉuṭaiya tamiḻ – pirāmi eḻuttukkaḷiliruntu tamiḻ varivaṭivam uruvākkappaṭṭatu” eṉpatākum. ikkaruttu ēṟkattakkatāka illai. kāraṇam mēṟcoṉṉa ki.mu. 300 cārnta tamiḻ nūlkaḷ, pāṭalakaḷ uruppeṟuvataṟku cila pala nūṟu āṇṭukaḷukku muṉṉarē tamiḻ varivaṭivamum ilakkiyaṅkaḷum tōṉṟat toṭaṅkiyirukka vēṇṭum. koṟkaiyil 1970 il akaḻnteṭutta pāṉaiōṭṭil uḷḷa tamiḻ eḻuttiṉ kālam ki.mu.700 eṉa appoḻutē uṟutiyākkap paṭṭatu. koṭumaṇalil aṇmaiyil akaḻnta pāṉai ōṭṭu eḻuttukkaḷiṉ kālamum ki.mu.500kku muntiyatāka irukka vēṇṭum eṉa irājaṉ (2004) niṟuviyuḷḷār. paḻaṉi arukē poruntal ūril kiṭṭiya poṟippukaḷ iraṇṭiṉ kālam ki.mu. 490 / 450 eṉa 2011 iṟutiyil nirṇayikkap paṭṭuḷḷaṇa (The Hindu  15-10-11). āticcanallūril 2005il naṭanta akaḻvāyvil kaṇṭa pāṉai ōṭṭup poṟippiṉ kālamum karimak kaṇippiṉpaṭi ki.mu.700 eṉavum ceyti vantatu. ivaṟṟaiyum piṟa ātāraṅkaḷaiyum cuṭṭi irājaṉ (2004), naṭaṉakācinātaṉ (2004) ākiyōr “tamiḻukku ki.mu.800ai oṭṭiyē oru taṉi vari vaṭivam iruntirukka vēṇṭum. ataṉaippiṉpaṟṟiyē acōka tamiḻ - pirāmi varivaṭivam (lipi) ki.mu. 300 il uruvākkappaṭṭirukkum” eṉpataṟkāṉa valuvāṉa kāraṇaṅkaḷai muṉvaittuḷḷaṉar. cintu nākarika lipi tirāviṭa moḻi cārntatu āṉapaṭiyāl pirāmi lipiyum atiliruntē uruvākiyirukka vēṇṭum eṉpar hīrās New Review;  cūlai (1936).

41. intiya moḻikaḷil tamiḻ oṉṟil tāṉ eḻuttukkaḷ neṭuṅkaṇakku nilaiyai aṭaivataṟku muṉṉaiya kālaṅkaḷil peṟṟirunta (cittira eḻuttu, uru eḻuttu, uṇarvu eḻuttu pōṉṟa) uruvaṅkaḷiṉ ilakkaṇam kūṟap paṭṭuḷḷatu. icceytikaḷai yellām virivāka ti.nā.cuppiramaṇiyaṉ taṉatu ‘paṇṭait tamiḻ eḻuttukkaḷ’ (1938) nūlil tantuḷḷatuṭaṉ, intiya eḻuttu muṟaikaḷ ellām tamiḻ eḻuttiliruntē tōṉṟiyirukkalām eṉpar.

“pirāmi lipi mutalil vaṭamoḻikkāka ēṟpaṭavillai yeṉṟum uyir eḻuttukkaḷuḷ a, i, u ākiya mūṉṟukkum atikac ciṟappaiyaḷippatum, meyyeḻuttukkaḷuḷ varkka eḻuttukkaḷaik koḷḷātatumāṉa oru pāṣaikkeṉa amaikkap paṭṭup piṉṉāl vaṭamoḻikku upayōkappaṭum paṭi putiya kuṟikaḷ uṇṭākkappaṭṭaṉaveṉṟum eṇṇavēṇṭiyirukkiṟatu. intiyāvil uḷḷa moḻikaḷuḷ ivvitam uḷḷatu tamiḻ oṉṟutāṉ. aṅṅaṉamāyiṉ pirāmi mutalil tamipakkeṉa amaintalipiyāka iruttal kūṭumā? iḵtu ārāytaṟkuriyatu”.

vaṭanāṭṭu varalāṟṟiñar es. ār. kōyal (1979) karuttum acōka pirāmi eḻuttu paṭippaṭiyāka (vaṭanāṭṭil) uruvāṉatalla; atu oru kālakaṭṭattil (ēṟattāḻa ki.mu.300
eṉakkūṟalām) putitāka uruvākkappaṭṭatu eṉpatākum. 

yāḻppāṇam āṉaik kōṭṭaiyil 1980l intirapālā kaṇṭeṭutta muttirait takaṭṭil piṉvarumāṟu “tīvukō” eṉṉum col cintu eḻuttilum, piṟkālat tamiḻ eḻuttilum amaintuḷḷatu eṉpar mativāṇaṉ:-

tī 	vu 	kō

cintuveḷi eḻuttu
tamiḻi

itiliruntu cintu veḷi acaiyeḻuttukkaḷ (Syllabic) tol tamiḻ eḻuttu vaṭivaṅkaḷāka intiyāveṅkum (īḻattilum) vaḻaṅkiṉa eṉṟum piṉ iṭai / kaṭaic caṅkat tamiḻp pulavar avaṟṟait tirutti “mutaleḻuttu” eṉṉum peyaril neṭuṅkaṇakkāka (Alphabetic) putiya eḻuttu muṟaiyai ki.mu.1800-1700 ai oṭṭi uruvākkiyirukka vēṇṭum; atuvē piṟkāla “tamiḻi”; atiliruntu vaṭa intiyarkaḷ “pirāmi” eṉṟa peyaril tam moḻikku eḻuttukaḷai ākkik koṇṭaṉar eṉpatu mativāṇaṉ karuttu. itaṉ virivai avaruṭaiya “Indus script among Dravidian Speakers”  (1995) nūlil kāṇka. kīḻavālai mutaliya iṭaṅkaḷil uḷḷa pāṟai ōviya eḻuttukaḷ pōṉṟu teṉṉāṭṭil vaḻaṅkiya palvēṟu tol eḻuttukaḷum cintu veḷi eḻuttukaḷuṭaṉ toṭarpuṭaiyavai eṉpatai annūlil viḷakkukiṟār.

42. ṭākṭar kē.vi. ramēṣ eḻuti ICHR (teṉ maṇṭalam peṅkaḷūr) 2006il veḷiyiṭṭuḷḷa “Indian inscriptions - a study in comparison and contrast”  eṉṉum nūl teḷivāka tamiḻi (tamiḻ - pirāmi) lipi kaṇṭippāka acōkaṉukku muntiyatu; anta lipiyaic caṟṟē māṟṟi acōkaṉ kalveṭṭu lipi uruvākkappaṭṭirukka vēṇṭum eṉpatu valuvāṉa ātāraṅkaḷuṭaṉ viḷakkukiṟatu. annūliṉ (pak 7-8) muṭivu varumāṟu:

(1) acōkaṉ kālattukku vekukālattukku muṉṉarē teṉṉintiyāviṉ teṉkōṭiyil pāṇṭiya nāṭṭil paḻantamiḻiṉ oliyaṉkaḷukku phonemes arumaiyākap poruntum tamiḻi lipi uruvākip puḻakkattil iruntatu. (kaṟārāka evvaḷavu kaḻipaḻaṅkālattil iruntu anta lipi vaḻaṅki vantatu eṉpatai niṟuva ituvaraic cāṉṟukaḷ kiṭṭavillai).

(2) vaṭa ilaṅraika aṉurātapuram akaḻvil kaṇṭa (ki.mu. 5-4 nūṟṟāṇṭukaḷ cārnta pāṉaiōṭu oṉṟil (intō - āriya varkka eḻuttu mutaliya oliyaṉkaḷ etuvum varāta) aramā eṉṟa puttamataccol tamiḻi lipiyil eḻutappaṭṭuḷḷatu. oricā - āntirak karaikkum vaṭa ilaṅkaikkum iṭaiyil akkālattil (ēṉ ataṟku neṭuṅkālam muṉaṉar iruntē) vaṇikam ceytu vanta kappal vaṇikar vacittu vanta iṭattil tāṉ avvakaḻvu naṭantatu. [camaṇar pannavana cutta kuṟippiṭum 18 paḻaiya lipikaḷil tamiḻi (Damili: piṉṉar itu Dramila  - >  Dravida eṉṟu urumāṟiyatu) polinti Polindi ākiyavaiyum uḷḷaṉa. tamiḻiyaic caṟṟē māṟṟi akkappal vaṇikar uruvākkik koṇṭatē polinti].

(3) antak kappal vaṇikarkaḷ tām tamiḻi (polinti) lipiyai mutaṟkaṇ teluṅkuppakutiyil paṭṭiprōluvukkum piṉṉar vaṅkāḷam, iṉṟaiya uttarap piratēcattiṉ kīḻaippakuti, rājastāṉ eṉṟavāṟu paṭippaṭiyākap parappiṉar. avvāṟu parappumpoḻutu pirākirutam, camaskirutam ākiyavaṟṟukkut tēvaippaṭṭa varkka eḻuttukaḷ mutaliyavaṟṟukkāṉa vaṭivaṅkaḷaiyum paṭippaṭiyāka uruvākkik koṇṭaṉar.

(4) mēṟcoṉṉa tamiḻi - polinti lipiyait tamatu poṟippukaḷukkup payaṉpaṭuttiya `acōkaṉ āṇaik kalveṭṭup poṟippāḷarkaḷ’ -- avvācakaṅkaḷai uruvākki, eḻutic cetukkiyavarkaḷ -- vaṭa intiya (pirākiruta - camskiruta) moḻikaḷukkut tēvaiyāṉa pala mukkiyamāṉa māṟṟaṅkaḷait tamiḻi lipiyil ceytu koṇṭaṉar. āṅkila mūlam varumāṟu:

1. There was the Damili script, fully answering to the phonetic needs of ancient Tamil in use in the Pandyan tract of the extreme south of the peninsula even before the days of Asoka, though we have, at present, no means of asserting how much before.

2. The script is found used, without the need for using any Indo – Aryan phonetic symbols, not found in ancient Tamil, for writing out a three - letter word arama, of Buddhist import on a potsherd found at the 5th - 4th century B.C. levels, of an excavated site in northern Sri Lanka, which sherd should have belonged to the boat people who were plying between the eastern coastline of North India and northern Sri Lanka.

3. Those very boat people carried this Damili (Polindi) script first to Bhattiprolu and then to Bengal, eastern UP and Rajasthan, all the time displaying gradual transformations as demanded by Prakrit and Sanskrit phonetics.

4. It is this script that the composers, writers and engravers of the Asokan edicts used in their writings, introducing in that process, many far – reaching changes and developments which we find in the North Indian vernacular script. 

43. tamiḻnāṭṭil irunta ki.mu.1900 - ki.mu. 300 cārnta peruṅkaṟkāla nākarikattiṉ toṭarcciyē caṅka ilakkiya kālam; annākarikak kūṟukaḷiṟ perumpālāṉavai caṅka ilakkiyattil kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa eṉpatum rājaṉ pōṉṟavarkaḷuṭaiya aṭutta muṭivākum. paltuṟai āyvukaḷ mēlum vaḷarum poḻutu
tolkāppiyattiṉ kālam ki.mu. 300kkum cila nūṟṟāṇṭukaḷ muṟpaṭṭatu eṉa aṉaivarum ēṟṟuk koḷḷum vāyppuḷḷatu.

44. ittaruṇattil iṉṉoṉṟaiyum kūṟa vēṇṭum. ki.mu.300kku muṟpaṭṭa caṅka ilakkiyaṅkaḷ evaiyumē namakkuk kiṭaikkavillai. ki.mu.300 - ki.pi. 300 kālakaṭṭattaic cārnta caṅka ilakkiyaṅkaḷum muḻumaiyāka namakkuk kiṭaikkavillai.

ki.pi.300ai oṭṭic caṅka ilakkiyaṅkaḷait tokuttavarkaḷ evvevaṟṟai viṭṭu vaikkalām eṉṟu karutiṉārkaḷō avaṟṟai maṭṭum tokuttuviṭṭu maṟṟavaṟṟai nīkkiviṭṭaṉar. tokuttavaṟṟilum māṟṟaṅkaḷ ceytirukkalām. nuṇṇaṟi vuṭaiyavarkaḷ itupaṟṟi 1871-liruntu terivittuḷḷavaṟṟaik kāṇpōm:-

cārlas ī kōvar: teṉṉintiya nāṭṭār pāṭalkaḷ The Folk songs of Southern India) 1871: tiṭṭamiṭṭuc citaikkappaṭāta allatu māṟṟiyeḻutappaṭāta tolpaḻaṅkālat tamiḻ nūl etaiyum iṉṟu kāṇpatu aritu. tamiḻ makkaḷ - tirāviṭa (paḻantamiḻ) ilakkiyattaik kaiviṭṭu purāṇakkataikaḷai nampiṉāltāṉ taṅkaḷukkup piḻaippu uṇṭu eṉṟu uṇarntavarkaḷē ivvāṟu paḻantamiḻ ilakkiyattaic citaittavarkaḷ āvar. (taṉit) tamiḻc ceyyuḷ nūlkaḷ pala matippiḻantu oḻiyumpaṭi avarkaḷ ceytaṉar. aṟavē oḻikka muṭiyāta nērvukaḷilum annūlkaḷai ayōkkiyat taṉamākac citaittu urumāṟṟi ulavaviṭṭu mūla nūlkaḷiṉ karuttai uṇara muṭiyātavāṟu ceytuviṭṭaṉar. civattampi (1986)yum ikkaruttiṉarē.

hayūnevil:ti tapropēṉiyaṉ (The Taprobanian) cūṉ 1896 “maturait tamiḻc caṅkamum ataṉ nūlakamum aḻikkap paṭṭapoḻutu paḻantamiḻ nūlkaḷ cakaṭṭumēṉikku aḻikkap paṭṭaṉa. (avaṟṟuḷ camaṇak koḷkai vāṭai irukkalām eṉṟa karuttil). īḻattilmakāvamcam iruntatupōl pāṇṭiyarkaḷ varalāṟṟaik kūṟum oru nūl maturaic caṅka nūlkaḷil oṉṟāka iruntaaḻikkappaṭṭu ataṟkup patilāka tiruviḷaiyāṭaṟ purāṇam uruvākkappaṭṭu irukka vēṇṭum. aḻikkap paṭṭa varalāṟṟu nūliṉ citaivukaḷuṭaṉ, aiṅkamarkaḷ (pārppaṉiya civa matattavarkaḷ) eṇṇam, camayamākiyavaṟṟukkup poruttamāṉa puṉai kataikaḷuṭaṉ piṉṉip piṇaintu appurāṇam uruvākkappaṭṭatu.”

jāṉ spiyars “ñāṉam tōṉṟiya iṭattiliruntu” vālyūs Values II - 10 cūlai 1957 : “cintuveḷinākarika muttiraikaḷil nūṟṟukkaṇakkāṉa (toltamiḻ) vācakaṅkaḷ uḷḷaṉa. kaṭal koṇṭa (aḻintupōṉa) paṇṭai nūlkaḷaip paṟṟit tamiḻ nūlkaḷ kuṟippiṭukiṉṟaṉa. eṉiṉum puttar kālamākiya ki.mu.600kku muntiya nūlkaḷuḷ oṉṟukūṭa kiṭaikkātataṟkāṉa kāraṇattai nām uṇara muṭikiṟatu. (tol tamiḻaruṭaiya) paṇṭaiya āvaṇaṅkaḷai āriyarkaḷ oṉṟu viṭāmal aḻittuviṭṭaṉar eṉpatē atu”


cintu muttirai varivaṭivaṅkaḷai intō-āriya (allatu vēṟu iṉa) moḻiyākap paṭittuḷḷōr muyaṟcikaḷum; avai eḻuttu, coṟkaḷaik kāṭṭum “lipiyē alla” eṉṟu kūṟum cilaruṭaiya nilaiyum.

45. cintu muttirai eḻuttukaḷai intō āriya moḻiyākavō, vēṟu iṉa moḻiyākavō, ōviyap pāṭaṅ kaṟpikka utavum karuvikaḷ pōṉṟavaiyākavō karutum vinōtamāṉa uṉṉippukkaḷ varumāṟu:

1) cuvāmi caṅkarāṉantā (1943): camskiruta tantira nūl vācakaṅkaḷākap paṭittuḷḷār.

2) pi.em.pāruvā : tantira vācakaṅkaḷē muttiraikaḷil uḷḷaṉa.

3) ṭi.eṉ.rāmaccantiraṉ (1956) : vēḷvikaḷil tarum taṭciṇaikaḷukkāṉa ṭōkkaṉkaḷ tāṉ immuttiraikaḷ vēḷvik kāṭcikaḷē muttiraikaḷil uḷḷaṉa.

4) ār.em.nāt (ṣillāṅ) 1959 : muttiraikaḷ tāyattu entiraṅkaḷ tām.

5) es.kē.rē : paḷḷikaḷil ilakkaṇam kaṟṟuk koṭukkap payaṉpaṭṭa villaikaḷ tām ivai.

6) ālaṉ es.ci.rās (1937) : “mokañcotarō eḻuttiṉ eṇ kuṟiyīṭukaḷ” (The Numeral signs of the Mohenjadaro script)) eṉṉum taṉatu nūlil ivai toṉmaiyāṉa intōṉēciya moḻiyil eḻutappaṭṭirukkalām eṉṟār.

7) ṭākṭar eṉ jā (1995) : “cintu muttiraikaḷil vētac coṟkaḷ” (Vedic glossary on indus seals) 

8) ē.cuvāmi nātaṉ (1995) : “kalaik kaṇṇōkkil cintu veḷik kuṟiyīṭukaḷ”. ikkuṟiyīṭukaḷ eḻuttukkaḷē alla. ōviyam, naṭaṉam, ciṟpam ākiyavaṟṟai māṇavarkaḷukku kaṟpikka utaviyāka uruvākkiyavai eṉkiṟār.

9) pi.vi.cupparāyappā (1996) : “cintu veḷi eḻuttu iyalpum amaippum” “Indus script, its nature and structure” - ikkuṟiyīṭukaḷ vēḷāṇ poruḷ uṟpatti, payaṉpāṭṭu vivaraṅkaḷaik kāṭṭum kaṇakku villaikaḷ.

10) es.ār.rāv : ivar 1982 lum 1991lum veḷiyiṭṭa nūlkaḷil cintu veḷi muttiraikaḷil camskiruta vācakaṅkaḷākap paṭittuḷḷa cila varumāṟu. 

bhag - rk  pāka rk (pāka arkka = tēva arkka) 
bag - bhag - rk - a – ha  pak pāk rk aha (peruntēvaṉ arkka - viṉ muttirai) 
rama trida osa  rāma tirita ōca (virumpat takkatu, mūṉṟu vaḻikaḷil oḷi viṭuvatu)

11) ekparṭ rikṭar - aṣāṉs (Egbert Richter - Ushans) 1997 veḷiyiṭṭuḷḷa The Indus script and the Rg Veda āriyar ki.mu.1700-ai oṭṭi nuḻainta piṉṉar, āriyarallāta cintuveḷi nākarika makkaḷtām vētakāla camskiruta moḻikku uruk koṭuttaṉar; āriyarallāta (cintu veḷi makkaḷaic cārnta) riṣikaḷum piṟarum rikvēta slōkaṅkaḷai eḻutiṉar; avaṟṟil cila pala slōkaṅkaḷ cintuveḷi muttiraikaḷilum poṟikkap paṭṭavai; eṉṟavāṟu ivar ūkam celkiṟatu. inta aṭippaṭaiyil ivar pala muttiraikaḷil rikvēta slōkaṅkaḷaip paṭikkiṟār.

12) eṉ.jā maṟṟum eṉ.es.(2000) The Deciphered Indus Script - Methodology, Readings, Interpretations  ivarkaḷ cintup poṟippukaḷai vēta kāla camaskirutamākap paṭittuḷḷaṉar. ivarkaḷ muṭivukaḷ tavaṟu eṉpatai viḷakkum maikkēl viṭcel, sṭīv pārmar, askō parpōlā, ai.makātēvaṉ ākiyōr kaṭṭuraikaḷai Frontline  (ceṉṉai) 13-10-2000, 24.11.2000 itaḻkaḷil kāṇka.

46. vaṭa intiya āyvāḷar palar cintuveḷi nākarikam āriyar varukaikku muntiyatu eṉpatai ēṟka maṟuttu annākarikamum vētakāla nākarikamum oṉṟē eṉṟu vallaṭi vaḻakkāka muṭivu ceytu muttirai eḻuttukaḷai eppaṭiyāvatu vētakāla camskirutamākap paṭikka muyalvatai parpōlāvum (1994) kuṟippiṭṭuḷḷār. rāv paṭittuḷḷa vācakaṅkaḷaippaṟṟi kamil svelapil nakaiccuvaiyuṭaṉ kūṟiyatu (1985):

“cintu veḷi muttiraic ciṉṉaṅkaḷuḷ perumpālāṉavai colleḻuttukaḷ. āṉāl es, ār.rāv avaṟṟai eppaṭiyāvatu camskirutam ākap paṭittāka vēṇṭum eṉak karuti (uṭpiṇaippu nilai moḻiyākiya) camskirutattai oru vinōtamāṉa ‘ōracaic col moḻiyāka’ urumāṟṟam ceytuḷḷār. rāv paṭittavaṟṟaic cariyeṉṟu oppuk koḷḷa mūṭappaṟṟu maṭṭum iruntāl pōtātu. ōraḷavu mūḷaik kōḷāṟum uḷḷavarkaḷālēyē itaic cariyeṉṟu kūṟa muṭiyum”

iyal 10
varalāṟṟukkuc caṟṟumuntaiya (Protohistoric) kālat
tamiḻaka varalāṟu (ki.mu.1500 - ki.mu.300)

ēṟattāḻa intiyā muḻuvatumē tamiḻiya (tirāviṭa) nākarikamāka irunta varalāṟṟukku muntaiya Prehistoric / Protohistoric  kālamāṉa ki.mu.7000 - 1800 paṟṟi muṉ iyalkaḷil kaṇṭōm. avai perumpālum cintu maṟṟum vaṭamēṟku intiya kujarāt pakutikaḷaic maṭṭumē vivarittaṉa. (ēṉaiya pakutikaḷilum āṅkāṅku -- kuṟippāka kaṭalōrap pakutikaḷil -- cāṉṟukaḷ iṉi akaḻāyvu mutaliyavaṟṟil kiṭaikka vāyppu uṇṭu) tamiḻakattaip poṟuttavarai (ki.pi.300 varait “tamiḻakam” eṉpatu vēṅkaṭattiṟku teṟkiluḷḷa teṉṉāṭu muḻuvataiyum koṇṭatu).

2. muṉpattiyil kuṟitta parappilamainta tamiḻakattiṉ purōṭōhisṭari kālavaralāṟu ivviyalil curukkamāka tarappaṭukiṟatu. kāraṇam cintu nākarikamum ituvum oṉṟōṭoṉṟu toṭarpuṭaiyavai. [tirāviṭar ēṟṟattil Dravidian Ascent ki.mu.3000 ai oṭṭi cintup pakutiyil nākarikam ciṟappuṟa viḷaṅkiya kālattavai eṉṟu uṟutiyākat tolliyalāḷar ēṟkum cāṉṟukaḷ teṉṉintiyāvilum kiṭṭiṉāl (avai kumarikkut teṟkilum piṟa pakutikaḷilumuḷḷa (Continental Shift) kaṇṭattiṭṭup pakutiyilum kiṭṭalām) cintu nākarikak kālattilēyē atu pōṉṟa ciṟappuṟṟa nākarikam tamiḻnāṭṭilum viḷaṅkiyatu eṉa niṟuvak kūṭiya kālamum viraivil vara vāyppu uḷḷatu]. itupaṟṟiya cila varumāṟu.

3. tamiḻaka varalāṟṟil varalāṟṟuk kālat toṭakkam eṉa potuvāka iṉṟu ēṟkappaṭum caṅka kālam ki.mu.300 - ki.pi.300 cārntatu. ataṟku muntiya “varalāṟṟukku muntiya kālam” ākiya ki.mu.1000-300 paṟṟiya ceytikaḷait tiṟampaṭat taruvatu rājaṉ (2004) tolliyal nōkkil caṅkakālam ākum.  āticcanallūril uḷḷa ki.mu.1000 kālattiya iṭukāṭu 114 ēkkar parappuḷḷatu. atil 1 viḻukkāṭu maṭṭumē akaḻvāyvu ceyyappaṭṭuḷḷatu. koṭumaṇalil (caṅkakālak koṭu maṇam) 50 ēkkar parappuḷḷa paḻaiya vāḻviṭattilum 1 viḻukkāṭu maṭṭumē akaḻvāyvu ceyyappaṭṭuḷḷatu. inta oru viḻukkāṭu akaḻvāyvukaḷil kiṭaitta eccaṅkaḷiliruntē ki.mu.1000 mutal tamiḻakattil vāḻnta makkaḷē, iṭaiyīṭiṉṟi, caṅka ilakkiyam vivarikkum tamiḻar camutāya makkaḷāyiṉar eṉṟu niṟuva iyaṉṟuḷḷatu. makātēvaṉ (2003) Early Tamil Epigraphy nūlil tamiḻik kalveṭṭukaḷ, poṟippukaḷ evaṟṟaiyum ki.mu.200kku muntiyatākak karutavillai. āṉāl avaṟṟil pala iṉṟu ki.mu. 5 ām nūṟṟāṇṭu allatu ataṟkum muntiyavai eṉa niṟuvappaṭṭu varukiṉṟaṉa eṉpatai mēlē
kaṇṭōm.

4. ki.mu.1000 - ki.mu.300 kālakaṭṭamē tamiḻakattiṉ irumpukkālam allatu peruṅkaṟpaṭaic ciṉṉak (Megalithic) kālam eṉṟu kuṟippiṭappaṭukiṟatu. akkāla kaṭṭattil iṟantavarkaḷ tāḻiyil putaikkappaṭṭaṉar. kaṟpatukkaikaḷil aṭakkam ceyyappaṭṭaṉar. peruṅkaṟpaṭaic ciṉṉaṅkaḷ cilaviṭaṅkaḷil iruntaṉa; cila viṭaṅkaḷil illai; eṉiṉum ikkālam “peruṅkaṟpaṭaic ciṉṉakkālam’ eṉṟē potuvāka kuṟippiṭappaṭukiṟatu.

5. ki.mu.1000 aḷavil makkaḷ perumaḷavukku āṭumāṭu vaḷarppavarkaḷākavē iruntaṉar. vēḷāṇmai toṭakka nilaiyil iruntirukka vēṇṭumeṉpatu akaḻvāyvukaḷiṉ muṭivākum. koṭumaṇal, tāṇṭikkuṭi pāṉaiōṭṭut tamiḻiṉ poṟippuk kālam ki.mu.5 ām nūṟṟāṇṭākattāṉ irukka vēṇṭum eṉa rājaṉ niṟuviyuḷḷār. paḻaṉi arukē ‘poruntal’ eṉṉum ūril kiṭṭiya iraṇṭu poṟippukaḷ ki.mu. 490, 450 kālattavai eṉa niṟuvappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. (The Hindu  5-10-2011) appoḻutē eḻuttaṟivu peṟṟa camutāyam tamiḻakattil iruntatu. eṉavē varalāṟṟuk kālamē tamiḻakattil ki.mu.500liruntu toṭaṅkiyatākak koḷḷa iṭamuṇṭu eṉṟa rājaṉ karuttu valuvāṉatākum.

6. tamiḻiya (Dravida) moḻi pēcunar ki.mu.2000kku muṉṉar iruntē tamiḻakattilum teṉṉāṭṭilum iṭaiviṭātu vacittu vanta maṇṇiṉ maintarē eṉpataiyē tolliyal āyvukaḷ kāṭṭukiṉṟaṉa eṉpatai ālciṉ tampatiyar 1988 tam nūlil terivippatai iyal 1il kaṇṭōm.

7. cintuveḷi muttirai eḻuttukaḷiliruntē caṅkakālat tamiḻ eḻuttukaḷ uruvāyiṉa eṉpatu mativāṇaṉ, jīvā pōṉṟōr karuttu. [parpōlā pōṉṟōr itai ēṟpatillai.] ēṟattāḻa ki.mu.700kku muṉṉamē tamiḻukku ōr eḻuttu vari vaṭivam
iruntatu; ataip piṉpaṟṟiyē acōka pirāmi eḻuttu uruvākkappaṭṭatu; eṉak koḷvataṟkāṉa (kē.vi. ramēṣ (2006) uḷḷiṭṭōr tanta)ātāraṅkaḷai mēlē kaṇṭōm. 2006 pipravari mātattil mayilāṭutuṟai - cempiyaṉ kaṇṭiyūril vi.caṇmukanātaṉ kaṇṭeṭutta putukkaṟkālakkaruvi oṉṟil cintuveḷi eḻuttukkaḷ nāṉkiṉai ai.makātēvaṉ “muruku aṉ” (murukaṉ) eṉṟu paṭittuḷḷār. akkaruviyiṉ kālam ki.mu.2000- 1500 āka irukkalām eṉpar. putukkaṟkālat tamiḻ makkaḷum akkālattil vāḻnta cintuveḷi nākarika makkaḷum orē moḻiyaiyē pēciṉar; ammoḻi tirāviṭa moḻiyē eṉpatai ippoṟippu niṟuvukiṟatu eṉpatu avar muṭivu ākum. (The Neolithic people of Tamilnadu and the Indus Valley people shared the same language which can only be Dravidian and not Indo- Aryan; 1.5.2006. The Hindu).
